Night: Ortega vs. The Korean Zombie''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 180''''', '''''UFC on ESPN+ 38''''' and '''''UFC Fight Island 6''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on October 18, 2020, at the [[Yas Island#du Arena and Forum|du Forum]] on [[Yas Island]], [[Abu Dhabi]], United Arab Emirates.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/9/4/21423733/ufc-253-kicks-off-fight-island-return-abu-dhabi-september-26|title=UFC 253 kicks off Fight Island return in Abu Dhabi|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2020-09-04|access-date=2020-09-05}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/ufc/story/_/id/29814358/ufc-253-254-highlight-five-events-fight-island-return|title=Sources: UFC 253, 254 to be held at Fight Island|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2020-09-04|access-date=2020-09-05}}\n\n==Background==\n[[Behind closed doors (sport)|Without fans in attendance]], the promotion did not have to make consideration for the local timing of the event, so the plan was to proceed with normal timing for [[prime time]] hours on the [[Eastern Time Zone|east coast]] of North America. The main card was scheduled to begin at 3:00 am (October 18) local time in Abu Dhabi, with a full preliminary card beginning at approximately 12:00 am [[Gulf Standard Time]].{{cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/ufc-fight-island-where-ufc-fight-island-fight-island-schedule-and-more|title=UFC Fight Island - Everything you need to know|publisher=ufc.com|author=Press Release|date=2020-09-17|access-date=2020-09-17}}\n\nA featherweight bout between former [[UFC Featherweight Championship]] challengers [[Brian Ortega]] and [[The Korean Zombie|Chan Sung Jung]] served as the event headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/29690218/sources-brian-ortega-chan-sung-jung-headline-ufc-fight-night-oct-17|title=Brian Ortega-Chan Sung Jung to headline UFC Fight Night on Oct. 17|work=espn.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2020-08-15|access-date=2020-08-19}} The duo were previously scheduled to main event [[UFC Fight Night: Edgar vs. The Korean Zombie]] in December 2019.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2019/09/ufc-korea-brian-ortega-vs-chan-sung-jung-headliner-booked|title=Brian Ortega vs. Chan Sung Jung to headline UFC on ESPN+ 23 in South Korea|author=Nolan King|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|date=2019-09-23|access-date=2020-08-19}} However, Ortega pulled out of the fight with a knee injury and was replaced by former [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Frankie Edgar]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2019/12/5/20996673/brian-ortega-out-of-ufc-busan-main-event-against-korean-zombie-chan-sung-jung|title=Brian Ortega out of UFC Busan main event against 'Korean Zombie' Chan Sung Jung|author=Damon Martin|publisher=mmafighting.com|date=2019-12-05|access-date=2020-08-19}}\n\nA women's flyweight bout between former [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship|UFC Women's Strawweight Champion]] [[Jéssica Andrade]] and former [[UFC Women's Flyweight Championship]] challenger [[Jessica Eye]] was briefly linked to the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Jessica-Andrade-Moves-to-Flyweight-to-Take-on-Jessica-Eye-at-Oct-17-UFC-Card-174349|title=Jessica Andrade moves to flyweight, to take on Jessica Eye at Oct. 17 card|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Jay Pettry|date=2020-07-31|access-date=2020-08-24}} However, the pairing never materialized due to lingering injuries for Eye. Andrade faced another former flyweight title challenger in [[Katlyn Chookagian]] instead.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/08/ufc-jessica-andrade-vs-katlyn-chookagian-fight-booking-oct-17|title=UFC targets Jessica Andrade vs. Katlyn Chookagian for Oct. 17|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and John Morgan|date=2020-08-29|access-date=2020-08-31}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Ciryl Gane]] and [[Shamil Abdurakhimov]] was expected to take place at this event.{{cite web|url=https://boxemag.com/ufc/ufc-france/item/cyril-gane-vs-shamil-abdurakhimov-rebooke-pour-le-19-septembre.html|title=Cyril Gane vs Shamil Abdurakhimov rebooked for October 17|publisher=boxemag.com|author=Staff|date=2020-09-09|access-date=2020-09-09}} {{in lang|fr}}{{cite web|url=https://www.mmanews.com/shamil-abdurakhimov-vs-ciryl-gane-announced-for-sep-19/|title=Shamil Abdurakhimov vs. Ciryl Gane announced for Sep. 19|publisher=mmanews.com|author=Clyde Aidoo|date=2020-08-25|access-date=2020-09-04}} The pairing was originally scheduled for [[UFC 249]], but Gane was forced to pull out of the event after he suffered a [[pneumothorax]] in training.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/3/5/21166938/ciryl-gane-out-ufc-249-fight-shamil-abdurakhimov|title=Ciryl Gane out of UFC 249 fight with Shamil Abdurakhimov|date=2020-03-05|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|access-date=2020-07-15}} Subsequently, the pairing was rescheduled and cancelled on three other occasions for various reasons. First at [[UFC 251]], then briefly for [[UFC 253]] and [[UFC Fight Night: Covington vs. Woodley]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/with-sergei-pavlovich-out-ciryl-gane-vs-shamil-abdurkhaimov-targeted-ufc-event-sept-19|title=With Sergei Pavlovich out, Ciryl Gane vs. Shamil Abdurakhimov targeted for Sept. 19 UFC event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-07-15|access-date=2020-07-15}} The bout fell through once again as Abdurakhimov pulled out due to undisclosed reasons on September 28 and Gane was expected to face promotional newcomer [[Ante Delija]].{{cite web|url=https://lasueur.com/ciryl-gane-ufc-28-9-2020|title=UFC - New opponent for Ciryl Gane on October 17th!|publisher=lasueur.com|author=Staff|date=2020-09-28|access-date=2020-09-28}} {{in lang|fr}} However, that bout was also cancelled on October 14 due to contractual issues with Delija, seemingly related to his previous deal with the [[Professional Fighters League|PFL]].{{cite web|url= https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/10/ciryl-gane-vs-ante-delija-off-saturdays-ufc-on-espn-38-card|title= Ciryl Gane vs. Ante Delija off Saturday's UFC on ESPN+ 38 card|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun and John Morgan|date=2020-10-14|access-date=2020-10-14}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Renato Moicano]] and [[Magomed Mustafaev]] was scheduled for the event, but Moicano withdrew on September 15 for personal reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/9/15/21437794/renato-moicano-withdraws-ufc-fight-island-magomed-mustafaev|title=Renato Moicano withdraws from Fight Island clash with Magomed Mustafaev|author=Guilherme Cruz|publisher=mmafighting.com|date=2020-09-15|access-date=2020-09-15}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/9/23/21449583/renato-moicano-fight-island-lightweight-anthony-pettis-ufc|title=Renato Moicano explains why he's no longer fighting on Fight Island|author=Guilherme Cruz|publisher=mmafighting.com|date=2020-09-23|access-date=2020-09-24}} He was replaced by former [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#Featherweight championship|KSW Featherweight]] and [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#Lightweight championship|Lightweight Champion]] [[Mateusz Gamrot]].{{cite web|url=http://mma.uno/moicano-fuera-mateusz-gamrot-enfrenta-a-magomed-mustafaev-el-evento-del-17-de-octubre/|title=Moicano out, Mateusz Gamrot faces Magomed Mustafaev in the October 17 event|publisher=mma.uno|author=Steve Duncan|date=2020-09-16|access-date=2020-09-17}} {{in lang|es}} In turn, Mustafaev pulled out in early October due to undisclosed reasons.{{cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/vier-gevechten-gecanceld-voor-verschillende-ufc-evenementen-in-abu-dhabi/|title=Canceled four fights for various UFC events in Abu Dhabi|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2020-10-01|access-date=2020-10-01}} {{in lang|nl}}{{cite web|url=https://mma.pl/fen-30-wyniki-wazenia/|title=Mateusz Gamrot is waiting for the winner: I've fired the hardest part of it if it all happened|publisher=mma.pl|author=Maciej Święch|date=2020-10-02|access-date=2020-10-04}} {{in lang|pl}} Gamrot faced fellow promotional newcomer [[Guram Kutateladze (fighter)|Guram Kutateladze]] instead.{{cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/guram-kutateladze-vervangt-magomed-mustafaev-tegen-mateusz-gamrot/|title=Guram Kutateladze replaces Magomed Mustafaev against Mateusz Gamrot|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2020-10-06|access-date=2020-10-06}} {{in lang|nl}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] challenger [[Volkan Oezdemir]] and [[Nikita Krylov]] was briefly linked to the event. However, Oezdemir pulled out of the fight in early October citing a knee injury. A replacement could not be found and Krylov was removed from the card.{{cite web|url=https://fighttime.ru/news/item/22766-nikita-krylov-ne-budet-dratsya-na-bojtsovskom-ostrove-ufc-v-abu-dabi.html|title=Nikita Krylov will not fight on UFC Fight Island in Abu Dhabi|publisher=fighttime.ru|author=Staff|date=2020-10-08|access-date=2020-10-08}} {{in lang|ru}}\n\n[[Muslim Salikhov]] was expected to face [[Cláudio Silva]] in a welterweight bout at the event. However, it was announced on October 4 that Salikhov pulled out due to undisclosed reasons and was replaced by [[James Krause (fighter)|James Krause]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/10/4/21500976/with-muslim-salikhov-out-james-krause-steps-in-to-face-claudio-silva-at-fight-island-event-oct-17|title=With Muslim Salikhov out, James Krause steps in to face Claudio Silva at UFC's Fight Island event on Oct. 17|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2020-10-04|access-date=2020-10-04}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Krzysztof Jotko]] and [[Makhmud Muradov]] was briefly scheduled to take place at this event, before being moved two weeks later to [[UFC Fight Night: Hall vs. Silva]].{{cite web|url=https://sportyzive.cz/ufc/blizi-se-zapas-makhmuda-muradova-v-ufc/|title=The match of Makhmuda Muradova in the UFC is approaching|publisher=sportyzive.cz|author=Bohuslav Sedláček|date=2020-10-06|access-date=2020-10-06}} {{in lang|cs}}\n\n[[Mounir Lazzez]] was expected to face [[David Zawada]] in a welterweight bout at the event. However, it was reported on October 6 that Lazzez [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]] and would not compete. In turn, Zawada was removed from the card.{{cite web|url=https://www.thenationalnews.com/sport/other-sport/ufc-fight-night-mounir-lazzez-withdraws-after-testing-positive-for-coronavirus-1.1090000|title=UFC Fight Night: Mounir Lazzez withdraws after States\n|attendance= None ([[Behind closed doors (sport)|behind closed doors]]){{cite web|url=https://www.dazn.com/en-BR/news/mma/what-time-is-ufc-fight-night-derek-brunson-vs-edmen-shahbazyan-tv-channel-live-stream-schedule-odds/jkh0n5yji6461l5vpn2z1ldw6|title=What time is UFC Fight Night? Derek Brunson vs. Edmen Shahbazyan TV channel, live stream schedule, odds|publisher=dazn.com|author=Steven Muehlhausen|date=2020-08-01|access-date=2020-08-02}}\n|gate=\n|image= Brunson vs. Shahbazyan.jpg\n|previousevent= [[UFC on ESPN: Whittaker vs. Till]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC Fight Night: Lewis vs. Oleinik]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Brunson vs. Shahbazyan''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 173''''', '''''UFC on ESPN+ 31''''' and '''''UFC Vegas 5''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on August 1, 2020 at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/holly-holm-vs-irene-aldana-als-main-event-op-1-augustus/|title=Holly Holm vs. Irene Aldana as the main event on August 1|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2020-06-08|access-date=2020-06-12}} {{in lang|nl}}\n\n==Background==\nA women's bantamweight bout between former [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship|UFC Women's Bantamweight Champion]] [[Holly Holm]] and [[Irene Aldana]] was originally slated to serve as the event headliner. However, it was reported on July 22 that Aldana had [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]] and pulled out of the fight.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/7/22/21334843/irene-aldana-covid-19-positive-scratched-fight-with-holly-holm-at-aug-1-ufc-event|title=Irene Aldana: COVID-19 positive scratched fight with Holly Holm at Aug. 1 UFC event|author=Steven Marrocco|publisher=mmafighting.com|date=2020-07-22|access-date=2020-07-22}} As a result, Holm was removed from the card as well and the pairing was ultimately rescheduled for October, in the main event of [[UFC on ESPN: Holm vs. Aldana]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/ufc-on-espn-plus-31-brunson-vs-shahbazyan-to-headline-holm-vs-aldana-postponed|title=Brunson vs. Shahbazyan promoted to UFC on ESPN+ 31 headliner after Holm vs. Aldana postponed|author=Danny Segura|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|date=2020-07-22|access-date=2020-07-22}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.fightful.com/mma/irene-aldana-tests-positive-coronavirus-bout-holly-holm-reportedly-rescheduled-october|title=Irene Aldana tests positive for coronavirus, bout with Holly Holm reportedly rescheduled for October|author=David Tees|publisher=fightful.com|date=2020-07-22|access-date=2020-07-29}} Subsequently, a middleweight bout between [[Derek Brunson]] and [[Edmen Shahbazyan]] was then promoted to serve as a three-round main event. This fight was originally expected to take place in March at [[UFC 248]], but eventually moved to [[UFC Fight Night: Overeem vs. Harris]] one month later.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/derek-brunson-vs-edmen-shahbazyan-verplaatst-van-ufc-248-naar-ufc-portland/|title=Derek Brunson vs. Edmen Shahbazyan moved from UFC 248 to UFC Portland|author=Staff|date=20 February 2020 |publisher=mmadna.nl|access-date=2020-07-12}} {{in lang|nl}} However, that event was cancelled due to the [[COVID-19 pandemic]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/03/ufc-postpones-three-events-coronavirus-pandemic|title=UFC postpones three events amid growing coronavirus outbreak|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and John Morgan|date=2020-03-15|access-date=2020-07-12}} The pairing was rescheduled for this event.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/06/derek-brunson-vs-edmen-shahbazyan-booked-ufc-fight-night-august-1|title=Derek Brunson vs. Edmen Shahbazyan booked for UFC Fight Night event on August 1|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2020-06-08|access-date=2020-07-12}}\n\nA women's flyweight bout between former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Flyweight Championship|Invicta FC Flyweight Champion]] [[Jennifer Maia]] and [[Viviane Araújo (fighter)|Viviane Araújo]] was initially scheduled for June 27, at [[UFC on ESPN: Poirier vs. Hooker]].{{Cite web|url=https://globoesporte.globo.com/combate/noticia/ufc-acerta-luta-entre-as-pesos-moscas-brasileiras-vivi-araujo-e-jennifer-maia-no-texas.ghtml|title=UFC sets fight between Brazilian flyweight Vivi Araújo and Jennifer Maia in Texas|publisher=globoesporte.globo.com|author=Raphael Marinho|date=2020-02-27|access-date=2020-03-11}} {{in lang|pt}} However, the bout was rescheduled in mid-June and moved to this event after both participants faced [[travel restrictions related to the COVID-19 pandemic]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/6/17/21294793/travel-restrictions-forces-ufc-move-jennifer-maia-viviane-araujo-august|title=Travel restrictions move Jennifer Maia vs. Viviane Araujo to Aug. 1 UFC event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz and Damon Martin|date=2020-06-17|access-date=2020-06-18}} Subsequently, Araújo was removed from the card on July 20 after testing positive for COVID-19 and replaced by [[Joanne Calderwood]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/7/20/21332023/ufc-joanne-calderwood-viviane-araujo-jennifer-maia-fight-after-positive-covid-19|title=Joanne Calderwood steps in to face Jennifer Maia after Viviane Araujo tests positive for COVID-19|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz and Damon Martin|date=2020-07-20|access-date=2020-07-21}}\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Vicente Luque]] and [[Randy Brown (fighter)|Randy Brown]] was initially scheduled for April 11, at UFC Fight Night: Overeem vs. Harris. However, that event was cancelled in mid-March due to the COVID-19 pandemic. The pairing was rescheduled for this event.{{cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/vicente-luque-vs-randy-brown-opnieuw-ingepland-ditmaal-voor-1-augustus/|title=Vicente Luque vs. Randy Brown rescheduled for August 1 this time|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2020-06-23|access-date=2020-06-23}} {{in lang|nl}}\n\nA women's bantamweight bout between [[Ketlen Vieira]] and former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Bantamweight Championship|Invicta FC Bantamweight Champion]] and [[UFC Women's Featherweight Championship]] challenger [[Yana Kunitskaya]] was initially scheduled for this event, but eventually moved to [[UFC Fight Night: Lewis vs. Oleinik]] a week later.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/ufc-shifts-ketlen-vieira-vs-yana-kunitskaya-to-aug-8-event|title=UFC shifts Ketlen Vieira vs. Yana Kunitskaya to Aug. 8 event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and John Morgan|date=2020-07-15|access-date=2020-07-15}}\n\nSouth Korean fighters [[Park Jun-yong]] and [[Jung Da Un]] were respectively expected to face [[Trevin Giles]] and [[Ed Herman]] at the event. However, Park and Jun were both removed from the bouts on July 23 due to alleged travel restrictions related to the COVID-19 pandemic. In turn, Giles would face [[Kevin Holland]], while Herman would face [[Gerald Meerschaert]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/kevin-holland-replaces-da-un-jung-meets-trevin-giles-at-ufc-event-on-aug-1|title=Kevin Holland replaces Jun Yong Park, meets Trevin Giles at UFC event on Aug. 1|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2020-07-23|access-date=2020-07-23}}{{cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/UFC-Releases-Brunson-vs-Shahbazyan-Bout-Lineup-for-Aug-1-Fight-Night-174223|title=UFC releases 'Brunson vs. Shahbazyan' bout lineup for Aug. 1 Fight Night|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Jay Pettry|date=2020-07-23|access-date=2020-07-23}} On the day of the event, Meerschaert was pulled due to testing positive for COVID-19 and his bout against Herman was cancelled.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/08/gerald-meerschaert-tests-positive-covid-19-ed-herman-fight-off-ufc-on-espn-plus-31/amp|title=Gerald Meerschaert tests positive for COVID-19; Ed Herman fight off UFC on ESPN+ 31|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2020-08-01|access-date=2020-08-01|archive-date=2020-08-06|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20200806163727/https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/08/gerald-meerschaert-tests-positive-covid-19-ed-herman-fight-off-ufc-on-espn-plus-31/amp|url-status=dead}} During the event, Giles fainted just moments before his walkout and therefore his bout against Holland was also cancelled.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/8/1/21351228/trevin-giles-faints-backstage-ufc-vegas-5-fight-against-kevin-holland-canceled|title=Trevin Giles faints backstage, UFC Vegas 5 fight against Kevin Holland canceled|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2020-08-01|access-date=2020-08-01}} It was later reported that [[Dana White]] personally called Herman to see if he could fight Holland, but Herman was already somewhere outside of the quarantine bubble and prohibited from re-entering.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/8/2/21351430/a-cursed-48-hours-puts-ufc-vegas-5-among-the-most-snake-bitten-events-in-ufc-history|title=A cursed 48 hours puts UFC Vegas 5 among the most snake-bitten events in UFC history|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2020-08-02|access-date=2020-08-03}}\n\n[[Luke Sanders]] was expected to face [[Chris Gutiérrez]] at the event. However, Sanders was removed from the bout in mid-July for undisclosed reasons and replaced by promotional newcomer [[Cody Durden]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/7/26/21338967/with-luke-sanders-out-chris-gutierrez-welcomes-newcomer-cody-durden-at-ufc-event-aug-1|title=With Luke Sanders out, Chris Gutierrez welcomes newcomer Cody Durden at UFC event Aug. 1|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2020-07-26|access-date=2020-07-27}}\n\n[[Timur Valiev]] was expected to make his promotional debut against [[Jamall Emmers]]. However, Valiev was removed from the card two days before the event for undisclosed reasons and replaced by Vincent Cachero.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/timur-valiev-out-of-ufc-on-espn-31-jamall-emmers-vs-vincent-cachero-in-the-works|title=Timur Valiev out of UFC on ESPN+ 31; Jamall Emmers vs. Vincent Cachero in the works|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2020-07-30|access-date=2020-07-31}}\n\nFormer [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] challenger [[Ray Borg]] was expected to face [[Nate Maness]] in a bantamweight bout at the event. However, Borg was removed from the fight on the day of the event's weigh-in for undisclosed reasons. Maness faced [[Johnny Muñoz Jr.]] in a featherweight bout.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/ufc-espn-plus-31-ray-borg-out-nathan-maness-to-fight-johnny-munoz|title=Ray Borg out of UFC on ESPN+ 31; Nate Maness expected to fight Johnny Munoz|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2020-07-31|access-date=2020-07-31}}\n\n[[Eric Spicely]] was expected to face [[Markus Perez]] at the event. However, Spicely was removed from the fight (and subsequently released from the promotion) on the day of the event's weigh-in for health issues related to his weight cut. He was replaced by Charles Ontiveros, in what would be a catchweight bout of 195 pounds.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/eric-spicely-out-of-ufc-on-espn-31-bout-vs-markus-perez|title=Eric Spicely out of UFC on ESPN+ 31 bout vs. Markus Perez; replacement found|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2020-07-31|access-date=2020-07-31}}{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/8/3/21352764/eric-spicely-released-from-ufc-after-dropping-out-of-fight-due-to-botched-weight-cut|title=Eric Spicely released from UFC after dropping out of fight due to botched weight cut|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2020-08-03|access-date=2020-08-03}} In turn, Ontiveros was deemed ineligible to compete and did not weigh in, thus cancelling the fight.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/ufc-on-espn-plus-31-weigh-in-results-brunson-vs-shahbazyan-live-stream|title=UFC on ESPN+ 31 weigh-in results: One fighter misses; Perez vs. Ontiveros canceled|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2020-07-31|access-date=2020-07-31}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Jonathan Martinez]] weighed in at 140.5 pounds, four and a half pounds over the bantamweight non-title fight limit. His bout proceeded at a catchweight and he was fined 30% of his purse, which went to his opponent [[Frankie Saenz]].\n\nDue to the many unexpected changes and last minute cancellations, the event took place with only eight bouts on the card, making it the smallest UFC event since [[UFC 177]] in August 2014.\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card (ESPN+)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Middleweight\n|[[Derek Brunson]] \n|def.\n|[[Edmen Shahbazyan]]\n|TKO (punches)\n|3\n|0:26\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Women's Flyweight\n|[[Jennifer Maia]]\n|def.\n|[[Joanne Calderwood]]\n|Submission (armbar)\n|1\n|4:29\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Welterweight\n|[[Vicente Luque]] \n|def.\n|[[Randy Brown (fighter)|Randy Brown]]\n|KO (knee and punches)\n|2\n|4:56\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Bobby Green]]\n|def.\n|[[Lando Vannata]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (30–26, 30–27, 30–27)\n|3\n|5:00\n}}\n{{MMAevent card|Preliminary card (ESPN+)|header=no}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Catchweight (140.5 lb)\n|[[Jonathan Martinez]]\n|def.\n|[[Frankie Saenz]]\n|TKO (knee and punches)\n|3\n|0:57\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight\n|[[Nate Maness]]\n|def.\n|[[Johnny Muñoz Jr.]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (29–27, 29–27, 29–27)\n|3\n|5:00\n|Muñoz was deducted a point in round 3 due to repeated groin strikes.\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight\n|[[Jamall Emmers]]\n|def.\n|Vincent Cachero\n|Decision (unanimous) (30–27, 30–27, 30–27)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Bantamweight\n|[[Chris Gutiérrez]]\n|vs.\n|[[Cody Durden]]\n|Draw (unanimous) (28–28, 28–28, 28–28)\n|3\n|5:00\n|First ever unanimous draw in UFC history without point deductions\n}}\n{{MMAevent end|notes=yes}}\n{{Cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/event/ufc-fight-night-august-01-2020|title=UFC Fight Night: Brunson vs. Shahbazyan|date=16 June 2020 |publisher=Ultimate Fighting Championship|access-date=2020-06-16}}\n\n==Bonus awards==\nThe following fighters received $50,000 bonuses.{{Cite web|date=2020-08-02|title=UFC Vegas 5 bonuses! Luque banks $50K for 11th UFC finish|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2020/8/1/21350907/official-ufc-vegas-5-bonus-winners-brunson-shahbazyan-results-maia-green-vannata-luque-mma-espn|access-date=2020-08-02|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Dan Hiergesell}}\n*'''Fight of the Night: Bobby Green vs. Lando Vannata'''\n*'''Performance of the Night: Jennifer Maia''' and '''Vicente Luque'''\n\n== See also ==\n{{Portal|Martial Arts|United States}}\n* [[List of UFC events]]\n* [[List of current UFC fighters]]\n* [[2020 in UFC]]\n\n== References ==\n{{Reflist}}\n\n{{UFC Events}}\n{{2020 in mixed martial arts}}\n\n[[Category:UFC Fight Night]]\n[[Category:2020 in mixed martial arts]]\n[[Category:2020 in sports in Nevada]]\n[[Category:August 2020 sports events in the United States]]\n[[Category:Ultimate Fighting Championship events in Enterprise, Nevada]]"}}}],"langlinks":[{"lang":"ja","title":"UFC Fight Night: Brunson vs. Shahbazyan"}]},{"pageid":63841819,"ns":0,"title":"UFC Fight Night: Figueiredo vs. Benavidez 2","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","content":"{{short description|UFC mixed martial arts event in 2020}}\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name= UFC Fight Night: Figueiredo vs. Benavidez 2\n|image=The_official_poster_for_Figueiredo_vs._Benavidez_2.jpg\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date={{start date|2020|07|19}} \n|venue= [[Yas Island#du Arena and Forum|du Forum]]\n|city = [[Abu Dhabi]], United Arab Emirates\n|attendance= None ([[Behind closed doors (sport)|behind closed doors]]){{Cite web|url=https://www.dazn.com/en-US/news/mma/ufc-fight-night-deiveson-figueiredo-vs-joseph-benavidez-time-tv-channel-live-stream-schedule-odds/1lm0304j5ggon17012e7l5khm7|title=What time is UFC Fight Night? Deiveson Figueiredo vs. Joseph Benavidez TV channel, live stream schedule, odds|publisher=dazn.com|author=Andreas Hale|date=2020-07-18|access-date=2020-07-19}}\n|gate=\n|previousevent=[[UFC on ESPN: Kattar vs. Ige]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC on ESPN: Whittaker vs. Till]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Figueiredo vs. Benavidez 2''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 172''''', '''''UFC on ESPN+ 30''''' and '''''UFC Fight Island 2''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on July 19, 2020 at the [[Yas Island#du Arena and Forum|du Forum]] on [[Yas Island]], [[Abu Dhabi]], United Arab Emirates.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/06/ufc-fight-island-located-yas-island-abu-dhabi|title=UFC 'Fight Island' is ... Yas Island, Abu Dhabi|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-06-09|access-date=2020-06-09}}\n\n==Background==\nThis event was the third of four UFC [[Fight Island]] events scheduled to take place on Yas Island in July 2020, following [[UFC 251]] and [[UFC on ESPN: Kattar vs. Ige]], as part of a plan to facilitate the hosting of events involving fighters impacted by U.S. [[travel restrictions related to the COVID-19 pandemic]].\n\n[[Behind closed doors (sport)|Without fans in attendance]], the promotion did not have to worry about the local timing of the event, so the event proceeded with normal timing for [[prime time]] hours on the [[Eastern Time Zone|east coast]] of North America. The main card began at 5:00 am (July 19) local time in Abu Dhabi, with a full preliminary card beginning at approximately 2:00 am [[Gulf Standard Time]].{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/29294243/ufc-251-know-know-fight-island|title=What we know and don't know about 'Fight Island'|work=espn.com|author=Jeff Wagenheim|date=2020-06-11|access-date=2020-06-23}}\n\nA [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] rematch for the vacant title between [[Deiveson Figueiredo]] and former title challenger [[Joseph Benavidez]] took place as the headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://www.thescore.com/mma/news/1978009|title=Benavidez-Figueiredo title bout set for UFC's July 18 main event|publisher=thescore.com|author=Nick Baldwin|date=2020-06-18|access-date=2020-06-18}} With former [[UFC Flyweight Championship|UFC Flyweight]] and [[UFC Bantamweight Championship|Bantamweight Champion]] [[Henry Cejudo]] vacating his flyweight title in December 2019, the pairing then met at [[UFC Fight Night: Benavidez vs. Figueiredo]] in February to determine the new champion. Figueiredo won via second-round TKO, but the title remained vacant because he missed weight.{{Cite web|url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/ufc-fight-night-169-results-deiveson-figueiredo-stops-joseph-benavidez-to-leave-flyweight-title-vacant/|title=UFC Fight Night 169 results: Deiveson Figueiredo stops Joseph Benavidez to leave flyweight title vacant|publisher=cbssports.com|author=Brent Brookhouse|date=2020-02-29|access-date=2020-06-18}} On July 11, it was announced that Figueiredo [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]], leaving the title bout in jeopardy. According to his manager, [[Wallid Ismail]], a second test was administered before an official decision was made regarding Figueiredo's possible removal from the event. Ismail said that Figueiredo already tested positive for COVID-19 two months ago and believes that this test was a false positive.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com.au/mma/story/_/id/29443075/deiveson-figueiredo-tests-positive-coronavirus-ahead-ufc-main-event|title=Figueiredo tests positive before UFC main event|work=espn.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2020-07-11|access-date=2020-07-11}} A day later, it was confirmed that Figueiredo passed his second test and would travel to Abu Dhabi, though he still needed to pass other tests upon arrival.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/7/12/21321857/deiveson-figueiredo-cleared-to-travel-to-fight-island-still-required-to-pass-more-covid-19-tests|title=Deiveson Figueiredo cleared to travel to 'Fight Island,' still required to pass more COVID-19 tests|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2020-07-12|access-date=2020-07-12}} He passed multiple tests in Abu Dhabi, clearing the fight for the main event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/7/17/21328086/deiveson-figueiredo-passes-multiple-covid-19-tests-cleared-fight-joseph-benavidez-vacant-ufc-belt|title=Deiveson Figueiredo passes multiple COVID-19 tests, cleared to leave quarantine ahead of weigh-ins|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2020-07-17|access-date=2020-07-17}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Sergey Spivak]] and [[Carlos Felipe]] was scheduled for the original May 9 date of [[UFC 250]] that later became [[UFC 249]], but was eventually scrapped due to that change. The pairing was rescheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=http://mma.uno/sergey-spivak-vs-carlos-felipe-transferido-a-fight-island-del-18-de-julio/|title=Sergey Spivak vs. Carlos Felipe transferred to Fight Island on July 18|publisher=mma.uno|author=Steve Duncan|date=2020-06-10|access-date=2020-06-18}} {{in lang|es}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between [[Roman Dolidze]] and [[Khadis Ibragimov]] was initially scheduled for [[UFC on ESPN: Eye vs. Calvillo|the promotion's inaugural event in Kazakhstan planned a month prior]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/breaking-khadis-ibragimov-treft-roman-dolidze-tijdens-ufc-nur-sultan-in-kazachstan/|title=Khadis Ibragimov meets Roman Dolidze during UFC Nur-Sultan in Kazakhstan|date=2020-03-03|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|access-date=2020-03-03}} {{in lang|nl}} However, the fight was postponed after the card was moved to Las Vegas due to travel restrictions for both participants related to the [[COVID-19 pandemic]]. The pairing was left intact and rescheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/06/ufc-targeting-roman-dolidze-vs-khadis-ibragimov-for-july-18-event|title=UFC targeting Roman Dolidze vs. Khadis Ibragimov for July 18 event|author=Nolan King|date=2020-06-11|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|access-date=2020-06-19}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Davi Ramos]] and [[Arman Tsarukyan]] was expected to take place at [[UFC Fight Night: Overeem vs. Harris]], but was postponed as the event was cancelled. The pairing was moved to this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/breaking-davi-ramos-vs-arman-tsarukyan-toegevoegd-aan-18-juli-evenement-op-yas-island/|title=BREAKING: Davi Ramos vs. Arman Tsarukyan added to the July 18 event on Yas Island|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2020-06-11|access-date=2020-06-12}} {{in lang|nl}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Marc Diakiese]] and [[Alan Patrick (fighter)|Alan Patrick]] was scheduled for the event. However, Patrick withdrew on June 14 for an unknown reason and was replaced by [[Rafael Fiziev]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/06/with-alan-patrick-out-ufc-targets-rafael-fiziev-vs-marc-diakiese-for-july-18|title=With Alan Patrick out, UFC targets Rafael Fiziev vs. Marc Diakiese for July 18|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Farah Hannoun|date=2020-06-13|access-date=2020-06-18}}\n\n[[Tagir Ulanbekov]] was expected to face Aleksander Doskalchuk in a flyweight bout at this event. However, Ulanbekov pulled out on July 3 after [[Abdulmanap Nurmagomedov]], father of current [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Khabib Nurmagomedov]], died from complications related to COVID-19.{{Cite web|url=https://fansided.com/2020/07/03/umar-numagomedov-tagir-ulanbekov-cancel-flights-fight-island-following-passing-khabibs-dad/|title=Umar Nurmagomedov, Tagir Ulanbekov delay travel to Fight Island following the passing of Khabib's dad|publisher=fansided.com|author=Amy Kaplan|date=2020-07-03|access-date=2020-07-04}} In turn, Doskalchuk pulled out a week later due to undisclosed reasons. They were replaced by [[Malcolm Gordon (fighter)|Malcolm Gordon]] and [[Amir Albazi]] respectively.{{Cite web|url=https://www.fightful.com/mma/report-malcom-gordon-replaces-tagir-ulanbekov-ufc-fight-night-figueiredo-vs-benavidez-ii|title=Report: Malcom Gordon replaces Tagir Ulanbekov at UFC Fight Night: Figueiredo vs. Benavidez II|publisher=fightful.com|author=David Tees|date=2020-07-04|access-date=2020-07-05}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/amir-albazi-vs-malcolm-gordon-ufc-july-18-fight-island-abu-dhabi|title=Amir Albazi steps in to face Malcolm Gordon at UFC's July 18 event in Abu Dhabi|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-07-10|access-date=2020-07-10}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card (ESPN+)}}\n{{MMAevent Stipe Miocic vs. Daniel Cormier 3 PPV schedule & main card start time|publisher=sportingnews.com|author=Andreas Hale|date=2020-08-15|access-date=2020-08-15}}\n|gate = \n|buyrate = 500,000{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/8/20/21378133/ufc-252-reportedly-sells-500000-pay-per-views-worldwide|title=UFC 252 reportedly sells 500,000 pay-per-views worldwide|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2020-08-20|access-date=2020-08-20}}\n|previousevent = [[UFC Fight Night: Lewis vs. Oleinik]]\n|followingevent = [[UFC on ESPN: Munhoz vs. Edgar]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 252: Miocic vs. Cormier 3''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on August 15, 2020, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.cleveland.com/sports/2020/06/stipe-miocic-vs-daniel-cormier-3-set-for-august.html|title=Stipe Miocic vs Daniel Cormier 3 set for UFC 252 in August|publisher=cleveland.com|author=Troy L. Smith|date=2020-06-09|access-date=2020-06-12}}\n\n==Background==\nThe promotion was initially targeting another event to take place on this date at [[3Arena (Dublin)|3Arena]] in [[Dublin]], [[Republic of Ireland|Ireland]].{{cite web|url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/ufc-returning-to-dublin-for-first-time-in-five-years-with-fight-night-event-in-august/|title=UFC returning to Dublin for first time in five years with Fight Night event in August|publisher=cbssports.com|author=Brian Campbell|date=2020-02-13|access-date=2020-06-24}} However, the organization confirmed in June that the Dublin card was indefinitely postponed due to the [[COVID-19 pandemic]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/6/24/21301631/ufc-officially-announces-cancellation-of-dublin-show-in-august-plans-return-for-near-future|title=UFC officially announces cancellation of Dublin show in August, plans return for 'near future'|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Newswire|date=2020-06-24|access-date=2020-06-24}}\n\n{{multiple image\n | align = left\n | total_width = 320\n | image1 = Stipe Miocic (48086643396) (cropped).jpg\n | width1 = 2300| height1 = 2900\n | alt1 =\n | caption1 =\n | image2 = Daniel Cormier.png\n | width2 = 2300| height2 = 2900\n | alt2 =\n | caption2 =\n | footer = Miocic ''(left)'' still holds the UFC heavyweight record of consecutive title defenses (three). Cormier ''(right)'' is the only fighter in UFC history to hold both the heavyweight and light heavyweight titles simultaneously.\n}}\n\nA [[UFC Heavyweight Championship]] trilogy between current two-time champion [[Stipe Miocic]] and former champion [[Daniel Cormier]] (also former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship|UFC Light Heavyweight Champion]]) took place as the event headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://au.sports.yahoo.com/stipe-miocic-vs-daniel-cormier-3-to-headline-ufc-252-on-aug-15-170549939.html|title=Stipe Miocic vs. Daniel Cormier 3 to headline UFC 252 on Aug. 15|publisher=au.sports.yahoo.com|author=Kevin Iole|date=2020-06-09|access-date=2020-06-12}} The pairing first met at [[UFC 226]] on July 7, 2018, where Cormier, as the light heavyweight champion, returned to heavyweight and captured the title by knocking Miocic out in the first round.{{Cite web|url=https://www.reviewjournal.com/sports/mma-ufc/daniel-cormier-ends-stipe-miocics-title-reign-at-ufc-226/|title=Daniel Cormier ends Stipe Miocic's title reign at UFC 226|publisher=reviewjournal.com|author=Adam Hill|date=2018-07-07|access-date=2020-07-12}} Their second meeting took place at [[UFC 241]] on August 17, 2019, where Miocic regained the belt and got his revenge via fourth-round knockout.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2019/08/18/ufc-241-results-stipe-miocic/|title=UFC 241 Results: Stipe Miocic reclaims title with unbelievable fourth-round KO|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Dan Doherty|date=2019-08-18|access-date=2020-07-12}} Cormier also announced that he planned on retiring after this bout.{{Cite web|url=https://bleacherreport.com/articles/2895411-daniel-cormier-says-he-will-retire-after-stipe-miocic-trilogy-at-ufc-252|title=Daniel Cormier says he will retire after Stipe Miocic trilogy at UFC 252|publisher=bleacherreport.com|author=Rob Goldberg|date=2020-06-09|access-date=2020-08-16}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Pedro Munhoz]] and former [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Frankie Edgar]] was briefly linked to [[UFC 251]], but promotion officials elected to schedule the pairing four days later for [[UFC on ESPN: Kattar vs. Ige]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.fightbookmma.com/update-pedro-munhoz-vs-frankie-edgar-moved-to-ufc-event-on-july-15th/|title=Pedro Munhoz vs. Frankie Edgar moved to UFC event on July 15th|publisher=fightbookmma.com|author=Ross Markey|date=2020-06-16|access-date=2020-06-18}} On July 6, it was announced that the bout between Munhoz and Edgar was scrapped after Munhoz [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/7/6/21315428/pedro-munhoz-tests-positive-covid-19-ufc-fight-island-frankie-edgar/|title=Pedro Munhoz tests positive for COVID-19, pulled from UFC 'Fight Island' bout with Frankie Edgar|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2020-07-06|access-date=2020-07-06}} They were later rescheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.thescore.com/news/1984875|title=Edgar-Munhoz rescheduled for UFC 252|publisher=thescore.com|author=Nick Baldwin|date=2020-07-12|access-date=2020-07-12}} However, promotion officials then moved the pairing again to serve as the main event of [[UFC on ESPN: Munhoz vs. Edgar]].{{Cite web|url=https://globoesporte.globo.com/combate/noticia/ultimate-remarca-pedro-munhoz-x-frankie-edgar-para-22-de-agosto-como-luta-principal-do-card.ghtml|title=Ultimate reschedules Pedro Munhoz vs. Frankie Edgar for August 22 as the main event|publisher=globoesporte.globo.com|author=Raphael Marinho|date=2020-07-27|access-date=2020-07-27}} {{in lang|pt}}\n\nA light heavyweight rematch between [[Magomed Ankalaev]] and [[Ion Cuțelaba]] was expected to take place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/rematch-na-controversiele-eerste-partij-tussen-magomed-ankalaev-en-ion-cutelaba-tijdens-ufc-252/|title=Rematch after controversial first game between Magomed Ankalaev and Ion Cutelaba at UFC 252|author=Staff|publisher=mmadna.nl|date=2020-06-12|access-date=2020-06-12}} {{in lang|nl}} The pairing first met at [[UFC Fight Night: Benavidez vs. Figueiredo]], where Ankalaev won via controversial knockout.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/2/29/21159660/ufc-norfolk-results-magomed-ankalaev-earns-ko-over-ion-cutelaba-in-highly-controversial-stoppage|title=UFC Norfolk results: Magomed Ankalaev earns KO over Ion Cutelaba in highly controversial stoppage|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2020-02-29|access-date=2020-07-12}} The bout was originally expected to take place at [[UFC 249]], on its original April 18 date. However, Ankalaev was forced to pull out of the event due to [[travel restrictions related to the COVID-19 pandemic]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/3/5/21167016/ion-cutelaba-vs-magomed-ankalaev-rebooked-for-ufc-249-following-controversial-stoppage|title=Ion Cutelaba vs. Magomed Ankalaev re-booked for UFC 249 following controversial stoppage|author=Damon Martin|publisher=mmafighting.com|date=2020-03-05|access-date=2020-07-12}} Cuțelaba then pulled out on August 11, as a result of testing positive for COVID-19 and the bout was rescheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Smith vs. Rakić]].{{Cite web|title=Ion Cuțelaba tests positive for COVID-19, rematch against Magomed Ankalaev bumped from UFC 252 to August 29 event|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/8/11/21364211/ion-cutelaba-positive-for-covid-19-ufc-252-rematch-with-magomed-ankalaev-off|access-date=2020-08-11|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco and Damon Martin|date=2020-08-11}}\n\n[[Rogério Bontorin]] was expected to face promotional newcomer [[Manel Kape]] at the event. However, Bontorin pulled out of the fight on July 22 due to an ankle injury.{{Cite web|author=David Tees|date=2020-07-22|title=Report: Rogerio Bontorin injured, off UFC 252|url=https://www.fightful.com/mma/report-rogerio-bontorin-injured-ufc-252l|access-date=2020-07-28|publisher=fightful.com}}{{Dead link|date=July 2025 |bot=InternetArchiveBot |fix-attempted=yes }} In turn, Kape announced his debut would be postponed and that he would not appear on the card either, also due to an ankle injury.{{Cite web|title=Manel Kape out of UFC 252 after suffering ankle injury, eyes October for debut|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/7/29/21346723/manel-kape-out-of-ufc-252-after-suffering-ankle-injury-eyes-october-for-debut|access-date=2020-07-29|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2020-07-29}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between Jorge Gonzalez and [[Ike Villanueva]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/ufc-252-jorge-gonzalez-vs-ike-villanueva-booked|title=UFC debutant Jorge Gonzalez meets Ike Villanueva at UFC 252 in Las Vegas|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2020-07-29|access-date=2020-07-29}} However, due to alleged [[Visa (document)|visa]] issues for Gonzalez, the pairing was pushed back and expected to take place a week later at [[UFC on ESPN: Munhoz vs. Edgar]].{{Cite web|url=https://mymmanews.com/ike-villanueva-vs-jorge-gonzalez-moved-to-aug-22-ufc-event/|title=Ike Villanueva vs. Jorge Gonzalez moved to Aug. 22 UFC event.|publisher=mymmanews.com|author=Connor Northrup|date=2020-08-13|access-date=2020-08-14}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Herbert Burns]] and [[T.J. Brown]] missed weight for their respective featherweight bouts. Burns weighed in at 149.5 pounds, three and a half pounds over the non-title fight limit. Brown weighed in at 146.5 pounds, half a pound over the non-title fight limit. Both of their bouts proceeded at a catchweight and they were each fined 20% of their individual purses, which went to their opponents [[Daniel Pineda (fighter)|Daniel Pineda]] and [[Danny Chavez]].{{Cite web|url= https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/08/ufc-252-stipe-miocic-daniel-cormier-weigh-in-results|title=UFC 252 weigh-in results: Title fight official, but two come in heavy|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2020-08-14|access-date=2020-08-14}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Heavyweight\n|[[Stipe Miocic]] (c)\n|def.\n|[[Daniel Cormier]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (49–46, 49–46, 48–47)\n|5\n|5:00\n|For the [[UFC Heavyweight Championship]].\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Bantamweight\n|[[Marlon Vera (fighter)|Marlon Vera]]\n|def.\n|[[Sean O'Malley (fighter)|Sean O'Malley]]\n|TKO (elbows and punches)\n|1\n|4:40\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Heavyweight\n|[[Jairzinho Rozenstruik]]\n|def.\n|[[Junior dos Santos]]\n|TKO (punches)\n|2\n|3:47\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Catchweight (149.5 lb)\n|[[Daniel Pineda (fighter)|Daniel Pineda]]\n|def.\n|[[Herbert Burns]] \n|TKO (elbows)\n|2\n|4:37\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Bantamweight\n|[[Merab Dvalishvili]]\n|def.\n|[[John Dodson (fighter)|John Dodson]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (30–27, 30–27, 30–27)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent card|Preliminary Card (ESPN / ESPN+)|header=no}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Vinc Pichel]]\n|def.\n|[[Jim Miller (fighter)|Jim Miller]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (29–28, 29–28, 29–27)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Women's Strawweight\n|[[Virna Jandiroba]]\n|def.\n|[[Felice Herrig]]\n|Submission (armbar)\n|1\n|1:44\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Catchweight (146.5 lb)\n|[[Danny Chavez]]\n|def.\n|[[T.J. Robert Whittaker vs. Darren Till TV channel, live stream schedule, odds|publisher=dazn.com|author=Andreas Hale|date=2020-07-25|access-date=2020-07-25}}\n|gate = \n|previousevent = [[UFC Fight Night: Figueiredo vs. Benavidez 2]]\n|followingevent = [[UFC Fight Night: Brunson vs. Shahbazyan]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC on ESPN: Whittaker vs. Till''''' (also known as '''''UFC on ESPN 14''''' and '''''UFC Fight Island 3''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on July 26, 2020 at the [[Yas Island#du Arena and Forum|du Forum]] on [[Yas Island]], [[Abu Dhabi]], United Arab Emirates.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/06/ufc-fight-island-located-yas-island-abu-dhabi|title=UFC 'Fight Island' is ... Yas Island, Abu Dhabi|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-06-09|access-date=2020-06-09}}\n\n==Background==\nThis event was the fourth and final UFC [[Fight Island]] event that took place on Yas Island, following [[UFC 251]], [[UFC on ESPN: Kattar vs. Ige]] and [[UFC Fight Night: Figueiredo vs. Benavidez 2]], as part of a plan to facilitate the hosting of events involving fighters impacted by U.S. [[travel restrictions related to the COVID-19 pandemic]].\n\nA middleweight bout between former [[UFC Middleweight Championship|UFC Middleweight Champion]] [[Robert Whittaker (fighter)|Robert Whittaker]] (also [[The Ultimate Fighter: The Smashes]] welterweight winner) and former [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] challenger [[Darren Till]] served as the event headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://www.fightmag.com.au/2020/05/29/robert-whittaker-vs-darren-till-to-headline-july-25-card-on-ufc-fight-island/|title=Robert Whittaker vs Darren Till to headline July 25 card on UFC Fight Island|publisher=fightmag.com|author=Parviz Iskenderov|date=2020-05-29|access-date=2020-06-13}} They were previously linked to an event in [[Dublin|Dublin, Ireland]] that was to take place in August 2020.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/3/11/21175401/robert-whittaker-vs-darren-till-targeted-for-ufc-dublin|title=Robert Whittaker vs Darren Till targeted for UFC Dublin|publisher=MMAFighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2020-03-11|access-date=2025-07-21}}\n\nThe event also included fighters that were pulled from other events previously cancelled, as well as the following bouts:\n*A light heavyweight bout between the former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship|UFC Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[Maurício Rua]] (also [[List of Pride champions#Tournament Winners|2005 PRIDE Middleweight Grand Prix Champion]]) and [[Antônio Rogério Nogueira]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/06/dana-white-ufc-books-antonio-rogerio-nogueira-vs-mauricio-rua-3-for-july-25|title=Dana White: UFC books Antonio Rogerio Nogueira vs. Mauricio Rua 3 for July 25|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2020-06-04|access-date=2020-06-18}} The pairing previously met at [[PRIDE Critical Countdown 2005]] and [[UFC 190]], with Rua winning both fights.{{Cite web|url=https://globoesporte.globo.com/combate/noticia/shogun-e-minotouro-se-enfrentam-pela-terceira-vez-no-ufc-250-em-sao-paulo.ghtml|title=Shogun and Minotouro face off for the third time at UFC 250 in São Paulo|publisher=globoesporte.globo.com|author=Staff|date=2020-02-20|access-date=2020-06-18}} {{in lang|pt}} The bout was scheduled for the original May 9 date of [[UFC 250]] that later became [[UFC 249]], but cancelled due to the relocation of the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/04/jose-aldo-brazilian-fighters-out-of-ufc-250-visa-issues-coronavirus-pandemic|title=Jose Aldo among Brazilians out of UFC 250 and beyond because of visa issues during pandemic|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2020-04-09|access-date=2020-06-18}} Nogueira announced that he planned to retire after this bout.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/antonio-rogerio-nogueira-retire-shogun-rua-trilogy-ufc-on-espn-14|title=Antônio Rogério Nogueira plans to retire after trilogy bout with 'Shogun' Rua at UFC on ESPN 14|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn and John Morgan|date=2020-07-21|access-date=2020-07-22}}\n*A women's bantamweight bout between former title challenger [[Bethe Correia]] and [[Pannie Kianzad]].{{Cite web|url=https://globoesporte.globo.com/combate/noticia/ultimate-planeja-remarcar-luta-entre-bethe-correia-e-pannie-kianzad-para-o-mes-de-julho.ghtml|title=Ultimate plans to reschedule fight between Bethe Correia and Pannie Kianzad for July, on Fight Island|publisher=globoesporte.globo.com|author=Marcelo Barone|date=2020-05-23|access-date=2020-06-18}} {{in lang|pt}} This bout was also scheduled for the original May 9 date of UFC 250 and removed for the same reasons as the fight above it.\n*A welterweight bout between [[Danny Roberts (fighter)|Danny Roberts]] and [[Nicolas Dalby]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/6/18/21295915/ufc-announces-full-lineups-for-july-events-on-fight-island-including-four-title-fights|title=UFC announces full lineups for July events on ‘Fight Island,’ including four title fights|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2020-06-18|access-date=2020-06-18}} They were scheduled to meet at [[UFC Fight Night: Woodley vs. Edwards]], but the event was the first of five to be cancelled due to the [[COVID-19 pandemic]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/03/ufc-dana-white-says-woodley-vs-edwards-being-rescheduled|title=Dana White: All called-off fights, including Tyron Woodley vs. Leon Edwards, are being rescheduled|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-03-24|access-date=2020-06-18}} However, Roberts was forced off the card due to injury and replaced by returning veteran [[Jesse Ronson]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/jesse-ronson-pakt-short-notice-partij-tegen-nicolas-dalby-op-25-juli-in-abu-dhabi/|title=Jesse Ronson takes short notice match against Nicolas Dalby on July 25 in Abu Dhabi|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2020-07-03|access-date=2020-07-03}} {{in lang|nl}}\n*A heavyweight bout between [[Tom Aspinall]] and [[Jake Collier]]. This fight was also scheduled for UFC Fight Night: Woodley vs. Edwards and removed for the same reasons as the previous one.\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Ramazan Emeev]] and [[Shavkat Rakhmonov]] was expected to take place at the event. However, Rakhmonov was forced to withdraw due to injury on July 2 and replaced by [[Niklas Stolze]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/7/2/21311421/danny-roberts-shavkat-rakhmonov-off-of-july-25-ufc-fight-island-card|title=Danny Roberts, Shavkat Rakhmonov off of July 25 UFC ‘Fight Island’ card|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2020-07-02|access-date=2020-07-03}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/niklas-stolze-vervangt-shavkat-rakhmonov-tegen-ramazan-emeev-op-25-juli-in-abu-dhabi/|title=Niklas Stolze replaces Shavkat Rakhmonov against Ramazan Emeev on July 25 in Abu Dhabi|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2020-07-05|access-date=2020-07-05}} {{in lang|nl}}\n\n[[Umar Nurmagomedov]] was expected to face [[Nathaniel Wood (fighter)|Nathaniel Wood]] in a bantamweight bout at this event. However, he pulled out on July 3 after his uncle, [[Abdulmanap Nurmagomedov]] (also the father of former [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Khabib Nurmagomedov]]), died from complications related to COVID-19.{{Cite web|url=https://fansided.com/2020/07/03/umar-numagomedov-tagir-ulanbekov-cancel-flights-fight-island-following-passing-khabibs-dad/|title=Umar Nurmagomedov, Tagir Ulanbekov delay travel to Fight Island following the passing of Khabib’s dad|publisher=fansided.com|author=Amy Kaplan|date=2020-07-03|access-date=2020-07-04}} He was replaced by promotional newcomer [[John Castañeda]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/john-castaneda-replaces-umar-nurmagomedov-vs-nathaniel-wood-ufc-fight-island|title=UFC taps John Castaneda to replace Umar Nurmagomedov, will fight Nathaniel Wood on 'Fight Island'|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Simon Head|date=2020-07-09|access-date=2020-07-09}}\n\nA women's strawweight bout between former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Strawweight Championship|Invicta FC]] and [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship|UFC Women's Strawweight Champion]] [[Carla Esparza]] and [[Marina Rodriguez (fighter)|Marina Rodriguez]] was scheduled for UFC on ESPN: Kattar vs. Ige nine days earlier. However, a Rodriguez [[cornerman]] [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]] and the bout was cancelled. Rodriguez had her test come back negative, but the bout was scrapped due to precautionary reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/marina-rodriguez-vs-carla-esparza-removed-from-july-15-ufc-event-after-positive-covid-19-test|title=Marina Rodriguez vs. Carla Esparza removed from July 15 UFC event after positive COVID-19 test|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura and Nolan King|date=2020-07-03|access-date=2020-07-04}} They were rescheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/carla-esparza-vs-marina-rodriguez-rebooked-july-ufc-abu-dhabi-fight-island-coronavirus|title=Carla Esparza vs. Marina Rodriguez reset for July 25 UFC show in Abu Dhabi|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2020-07-05|access-date=2020-07-05}}\n\nJustin Tafa was expected to face [[Raphael Pessoa]] in a heavyweight bout at this event. However, he pulled out on July 15 for unknown reasons and was replaced by [[Tanner Boser]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/ufc-fight-island-justin-tafa-out-tanner-boser-vs-raphael-pessoa-booked|title=Justin Tafa off July 25 UFC 'Fight Island' event; Tanner Boser expected to face Raphael Pessoa|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2020-07-15|access-date=2020-07-15}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Francisco Trinaldo]] weighed in at 160 pounds, four pounds over the lightweight non-title fight limit. His bout proceeded at a catchweight and he was fined 30% of his purse, which went to his opponent [[Jai Herbert]].{{Cite web|url= for the most on a single card in UFC history.{{Cite web|url=https://www.si.com/gambling/2020/07/24/ufc-fight-island-3-betting-dfs|title=UFC Fight Island 3: Robert Whittaker vs Darren UFC wins at 10 days (not including one-night-tournaments during the UFC's early days).{{Cite web|url=https://www.si.com/gambling/2020/07/24/ufc-fight-island-3-betting-dfs|title=UFC on ESPN 14 results: unknown}}\n\n==Aftermath==\nOn November 18, it was announced by the [[United States Anti-Doping Agency]] (USADA) that [[Jesse Ronson]] was suspended for 20 months after he tested positive for [[metandienone]] in an out-of-competition test conducted on July 22 and didn't disclose his use of the substance in his onboarding documents prior to re-joining the UFC. His suspension is retroactive to the test failure, meaning he'll be cleared to compete on March 22, 2022. They also announced that Ronson's victory was overturned to a no contest due to the violation.\n\n==See also==\n{{Portal|Martial Arts|UAE}}\n* [[List of UFC events]]\n* [[List of current UFC fighters]]\n* [[2020 in UFC]]\n\n==References==\n{{Reflist}}\n\n{{UFC Events}}\n{{2020 in mixed martial arts}}\n\n[[Category:UFC on ESPN]]\n[[Category:2020 in mixed martial arts]]\n[[Category:2020 in Emirati sport]]\n[[Category:Mixed martial arts events in Abu Dhabi]]\n[[Category:2020 in sport in Abu Dhabi]]\n[[Category:July 2020 sports events in Asia]]"}}}],"langlinks":[{"lang":"ja","title":"UFC on ESPN: Whittaker vs. Till"}]},{"pageid":64287862,"ns":0,"title":"UFC Fight Night: Lewis vs. Oleinik","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","content":"{{short description|UFC mixed martial arts event in 2020}}\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name= UFC Fight Night: Lewis vs. Oleinik\n|image=Lewis_vs._Oleinik.jpeg\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date={{start date|2020|08|08}}\n|venue= [[UFC Apex]]\n|city= [[Enterprise, Nevada]], United States\n|attendance=None ([[Behind closed doors (sport)|behind closed doors]]){{cite web|url=https://www.dazn.com/en-BR/news/mma/ufc-fight-night-derrick-lewis-vs-aleksei-oleinik-date-time-tv-channel-live-stream/1f0nhkl8xz92r1n3kbwzgvmubq|title=What time, channel is UFC Fight Night tonight? vs. Shahbazyan]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC 252|UFC 252: Miocic vs. Cormier 3]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Lewis vs. Oleinik''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 174''''', '''''UFC on ESPN+ 32''''' and '''''UFC Vegas 6''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] on August 8, 2020 at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|title=Derrick Lewis vs. Alexey Oleinik expected to headline UFC card on Aug. 8|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/6/16/21293471/derrick-lewis-vs-alexey-oleinik-expected-to-headline-ufc-card-on-aug-8|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2020-06-16|access-date=2020-06-17}}\n\n==Background==\nA heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Heavyweight Championship]] challenger [[Derrick Lewis]] and [[Aleksei Oleinik]] took place as the event headliner.\n\nA women's bantamweight bout between former [[UFC Women's Flyweight Championship|UFC Women's Flyweight Champion]] [[Nicco Montaño]] and [[Julia Avila]] was originally scheduled for the event.{{cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/nicco-montano-treft-julia-avila-na-drie-jaar-opnieuw-ditmaal-op-8-augustus-in-de-ufc/|title=Nicco Montaño will meet Julia Avila again after three years, this time on August 8 in the UFC|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2020-06-18|access-date=2020-06-18}} {{in lang|nl}} However, the bout was rescheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Overeem vs. Sakai]] after Montaño's coach, John Wood, [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/7/11/21321100/nicco-montano-vs-julia-avila-matchup-moved-to-ufc-sept-5-following-coachs-positive-covid-19-test|title=Nicco Montano vs. Julia Avila matchup moved to UFC event on Sept. 5 following coach's positive COVID-19 test|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2020-06-18|access-date=2020-07-12}}\n\nA women's bantamweight bout between [[Ketlen Vieira]] and former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Bantamweight Championship|Invicta FC Bantamweight Champion]] and [[UFC Women's Featherweight Championship]] challenger [[Yana Kunitskaya]] was originally scheduled for a week earlier at [[UFC Fight Night: Brunson vs. Shahbazyan]]. However, it eventually moved to this event instead.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/ufc-shifts-ketlen-vieira-vs-yana-kunitskaya-to-aug-8-event|title=UFC shifts Ketlen Vieira vs. Yana Kunitskaya to Aug. 8 event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and John Morgan|date=2020-07-15|access-date=2020-07-15}} Subsequently, Vieira was removed from the card on July 30 due to [[Travel visa|visa]] issues and replaced by returning veteran [[Julija Stoliarenko]], who was the current Invicta FC Bantamweight Champion.{{cite web|url=https://mymmanews.com/ketlen-viera-out-yana-kunitskaya-will-now-face-julija-stoliarenko-at-ufcs-august-8th-event/|title=Ketlen Viera OUT, Yana Kunitskaya will now face Julija Stoliarenko at UFC's August 8th event.|publisher=mymmanews.com|author=Lenny March|date=2020-07-30|access-date=2020-07-31}} Vieira was then expected to face a different opponent at [[UFC Fight Night: Waterson vs. Hill]] on September 12.{{cite web|url=https://globoesporte.globo.com/combate/noticia/sem-visto-para-os-eua-ketlen-vieira-fica-fora-da-luta-com-kunitskaya-em-las-vegas.ghtml|title=Without a visa to the USA, Ketlen Vieira is out of the fight with Kunitskaya in Las Vegas|publisher=globoesporte.globo.com|author=Staff|date=2020-07-31|access-date=2020-07-31}} {{in lang|pt}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Sergei Pavlovich]] and [[Ciryl Gane]] was expected to take place at this event.{{cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/sergei-pavlovich-treft-ciryl-gane-tijdens-8-augustus-evenement/|title=Sergei Pavlovich encounters Ciryl Gane during the August 8 event|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2020-04-14|access-date=2020-07-15}} {{in lang|nl}} However, Pavlovich pulled out on July 15 due to an old knee injury that required surgery. He was replaced by [[Shamil Abdurakhimov]] and the bout moved to [[UFC 253]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/with-sergei-pavlovich-out-ciryl-gane-vs-shamil-abdurkhaimov-targeted-ufc-event-sept-19|title=With Sergei Pavlovich out, Ciryl Gane vs. Shamil Abdurakhimov targeted for Sept. 19 UFC event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-07-15|access-date=2020-07-15}} Gane and Abdurakhimov were originally scheduled for [[UFC 249]], but Gane was forced to pull out of the event after he was struck by a [[pneumothorax]] in training.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/3/5/21166938/ciryl-gane-out-ufc-249-fight-shamil-abdurakhimov|title=Ciryl Gane out of UFC 249 fight with Shamil Abdurakhimov|date=2020-03-05|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|access-date=2020-07-15}} Subsequently, the bout would be rescheduled for [[UFC 251]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/shamil-abdurakhimov-vs-ciryl-gane-tijdens-ufc-251s-ifw-week-evenement/|title=Shamil Abdurakhimov vs. Ciryl Gane at UFC 251's IFW week event|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2020-05-10|access-date=2020-06-09}} {{in lang|nl}} However, it was then cancelled a second time as Abdurakhimov was removed from the card for undisclosed reasons.\n\nA featherweight bout between [[Steve Garcia]] and Peter Barrett was scheduled for the event, but Garcia withdrew from the bout for an undisclosed reason on July 25 and was replaced by [[Youssef Zalal]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/youssef-zalal-pakt-short-notice-gevecht-tegen-peter-barrett-op-8-augustus/|title=Youssef Zalal tackles short notice fight against Peter Barrett on August 8|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2020-07-25|access-date=2020-07-26}} {{in lang|nl}}\n\nA couple of fights, including a women's strawweight bout between [[Nadia Kassem]] and Miranda Granger, as well as a featherweight bout between [[Alex Caceres]] and [[Giga Chikadze]] were expected to take place at this event. However, they never made it to the final card for unknown reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/breaking-miranda-granger-vs-nadia-kassem-toegevoegd-aan-ufc-evenement-op-8-augustus/|title=BREAKING: Miranda Granger vs. Nadia Kassem added to UFC event on August 8|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2020-09-19|access-date=2020-06-18}} {{in lang|nl}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/bruce-leeroy-treft-giga-chikadze-tijdens-ufc-evenement-op-8-augustus/|title=\"Bruce Leeroy\" meets Giga Chikadze at UFC event on August 8|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2020-07-17|access-date=2020-07-18}} {{in lang|nl}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Beneil Dariush]] and [[Laureano Staropoli]] missed weight for their respective bouts. Dariush weighed in at 158 pounds, two pounds over the lightweight non-title fight limit. Staropoli weighed in at 174.5 pounds, three and a half pounds over the welterweight non-title fight limit. Both of their bouts proceeded at a catchweight and they were each fined 20% of their individual purses, which went to their opponents [[Scott Holtzman]] and [[Tim Means]] (Means was given one additional hour to make weight after initially weighing 172.5 pounds).{{Cite web|url= Edgar]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC Fight Night: Overeem vs. Sakai]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Smith vs. Rakić''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 175''''', '''''UFC on ESPN+ 33''''' and '''''UFC Vegas 8''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on August 29, 2020, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/06/ufc-aleksandar-rakic-anthony-smith-august-29|title=UFC adds Aleksandar Rakic vs. Anthony Smith to Aug. 29 lineup|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun and Mike Bohn|date=2020-06-19|access-date=2020-06-23}}\n\n== Background ==\nPromotion officials were originally targeting a featherweight bout between [[The Ultimate Fighter: Latin America]] featherweight winner [[Yair Rodríguez]] and [[Zabit Magomedsharipov]] to serve as the event headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://www.fightful.com/mma/report-yair-rodriguez-faces-zabit-magomedsharipov-august-29-ufc-event|title=Report: Yair Rodriguez faces Zabit Magomedsharipov at August 29 UFC event|publisher=fightful.com|author=David Tees|date=2020-07-30|access-date=2020-07-30}} However, Rodríguez pulled out of the bout on August 5 with an ankle injury and Magomedsharipov announced he would not fight at the event.{{Cite web|title=Dana White confirms Yair Rodriguez injured, out of fight vs. Zabit Magomedsharipov|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/08/dana-white-confirms-yair-rodriguez-injured-out-of-fight-vs-zabit-magomedsharipov|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2020-08-04|access-date=2020-08-05}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/8/10/21362152/zabit-magomedsharipov-announces-he-will-not-fight-on-aug-29-returning-to-russia-for-now|title=Zabit Magomedsharipov announces he will not fight on Aug. 29, returning to Russia for now|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2020-08-10|access-date=2020-08-11}} The pairing was first scheduled to take place at [[UFC 228]] in September 2018, but Rodríguez also pulled out of that event with an injury.{{Cite news|url=https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2018/8/23/17773928/injury-yair-rodriguez-ufc-228-fight-zabit-magomedsharipov-mma-news|title=Injury forces Yair Rodriguez out of UFC 228 fight vs. Zabit Magomedsharipov|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Harry Davies|date=2018-08-23|access-date=2018-08-24|archive-date=2020-11-09|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20201109010531/https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2018/8/23/17773928/injury-yair-rodriguez-ufc-228-fight-zabit-magomedsharipov-mma-news|url-status=dead}} The pairing was rescheduled for [[UFC 254]].{{Cite news|url=https://www.bjpenn.com/mma-news/ufc/report-zabit-magomedsharipov-vs-yair-rodriguez-rescheduled-for-ufc-254/|title=Zabit Magomedsharipov vs. Yair Rodriguez rescheduled for UFC 254|publisher=bjpenn.com|author=Cole Shelton|date=2020-08-14|access-date=2020-08-15}}\n\nOn August 15, it was announced that a light heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] challenger [[Anthony Smith (fighter)|Anthony Smith]] and [[Aleksandar Rakić]] would serve as a three-round main event.{{Cite news|url=https://www.thescore.com/mma/news/2003186|title=Smith-Rakic promoted to UFC main event on Aug. 29|publisher=thescore.com|author=Nick Baldwin|date=2020-08-15|access-date=2020-08-15}}\n\n[[Emily Whitmire]] was scheduled to face [[Polyana Viana]] in a women's strawweight bout at [[UFC 248]] earlier this year.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/emily-whitmire-treft-polyana-viana-tijdens-ufc-248-in-las-vegas/|title=Emily Whitmire faces Polyana Viana at UFC 248 in Las Vegas|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2019-12-28|access-date=2020-07-10}} {{in lang|nl}} However, Whitmire weighed in at 117.5 pounds, one and a half pounds over the strawweight non-title fight limit. She was subsequently hospitalized the day of the event, thus cancelling the fight.{{Cite web|url=https://www.yahoo.com/sports/ufc-248-preliminary-bout-scratched-194153259.html|title=UFC 248 preliminary bout scratched last minute due to medical concerns|author=Staff|date=2020-03-07|publisher=yahoo.com|access-date=2020-03-07}} The pairing was rescheduled for this event instead.{{Cite web|title=Emily Whitmire vs. Polyana Viana rescheduled for UFC's Aug. 29 event|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/emily-whitmire-vs-polyana-viana-rescheduled-ufc-august-29|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-07-10|access-date=2020-09-01}} It was reportedly rescheduled as a women's flyweight bout, but ultimately took place as a women's strawweight contest.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/tweede-poging-voor-emily-whitmire-vs-polyana-viana-ditmaal-in-de-flyweight-divisie/|title=Second try for Emily Whitmire vs. Polyana Viana, this time in the Flyweight division|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2020-07-10|access-date=2020-07-10}} {{in lang|nl}}\n\nA featherweight bout between former [[UFC Featherweight Championship]] challenger [[Ricardo Lamas]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team McGregor vs. Team Faber]] lightweight winner [[Ryan Hall (fighter)|Ryan Hall]] was also scheduled to initially take place earlier this year at [[UFC Fight Night: Hermansson vs. Weidman]], but the event was cancelled in mid-March due to the [[COVID-19 pandemic]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/4/9/21215721/ufc-249-upcoming-events-cancelled-due-to-coronavirus-dana-white-promises-fight-island-will-proceed|title=UFC 249, upcoming events postponed due to coronavirus pandemic, Dana White promises 'Fight Island' will proceed|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2020-04-09|access-date=2020-04-09}} The pairing was rescheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/7/3/21312305/ryan-hall-vs-ricardo-lamas-rebooked-for-ufc-event-on-aug-29|title=Ryan Hall vs. Ricardo Lamas rebooked for UFC event on Aug. 29|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2020-07-03|access-date=2020-07-03}} In turn, Hall pulled out due to undisclosed reasons a week before the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/08/ufc-ryan-hall-out-ricardo-lamas-fight-new-opponent-aug-29|title=Ryan Hall out of Ricardo Lamas fight at UFC's Aug. 29 event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2020-08-20|access-date=2020-08-21}} He was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Bill Algeo]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/08/ufc-on-espn-plus-33-bill-algeo-ricardo-lamas|title=With Ryan Hall out, Bill Algeo steps in to face Ricardo Lamas at UFC on ESPN+ 33|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-08-24|access-date=2020-08-24}}\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Neil Magny]] and [[Geoff Neal]] was scheduled to take place at this event. However, Neal withdrew from the event on August 9 due to a severe case of [[pneumonia]] that evolved to [[sepsis]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/8/10/21362784/coach-geoff-neal-expected-to-recover-after-mysterious-ailment-that-caused-pneumonia-sepsis|title=Coach: Geoff Neal expected to recover after mysterious ailment that caused staphylococcal pneumonia, sepsis|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2020-08-10|access-date=2020-08-11}} He was replaced by former [[UFC Welterweight Champion]] [[Robbie Lawler]]. The original pairing is now expected to take place at [[UFC on ESPN: Rodriguez vs. Waterson]]{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/08/neil-magny-vs-robbie-lawler-booked-aug-29-ufc-event-geoff-neal-health-scare|title=Neil Magny vs. Robbie Lawler booked for Aug. 29 UFC event after Geoff Neal suffers health scare|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Mike Bohn|date=2020-08-09|access-date=2020-08-09}}\n\nA light heavyweight rematch between [[Magomed Ankalaev]] and [[Ion Cuțelaba]] was expected to take place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/rematch-na-controversiele-eerste-partij-tussen-magomed-ankalaev-en-ion-cutelaba-tijdens-ufc-252/|title=Rematch after controversial first game between Magomed Ankalaev and Ion Cutelaba at UFC 252|author=Staff|publisher=mmadna.nl|date=2020-06-12|access-date=2020-06-12}} {{in lang|nl}} The pair first met at [[UFC Fight Night: Benavidez vs. Figueiredo]], where Ankalaev won via controversial knockout.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/2/29/21159660/ufc-norfolk-results-magomed-ankalaev-earns-ko-over-ion-cutelaba-in-highly-controversial-stoppage|title=UFC Norfolk results: Magomed Ankalaev earns KO over Ion Cutelaba in highly controversial stoppage|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2020-02-29|access-date=2020-07-12}} The bout was originally expected to take place at [[UFC 249]], on its original April 18 date. However, Ankalaev was forced to pull out of the event due to [[travel restrictions related to the COVID-19 pandemic]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/3/5/21167016/ion-cutelaba-vs-magomed-ankalaev-rebooked-for-ufc-249-following-controversial-stoppage|title=Ion Cutelaba vs. Magomed Ankalaev re-booked for UFC 249 following controversial stoppage|author=Damon Martin|publisher=mmafighting.com|date=2020-03-05|access-date=2020-07-12}} They were later rebooked for [[UFC 252]], but Cuțelaba then pulled out after he [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]] and the bout was rescheduled for this event.{{Cite web|title=Ion Cuțelaba tests positive for COVID-19, rematch against Magomed Ankalaev bumped from UFC 252 to August 29 event|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/8/11/21364211/ion-cutelaba-positive-for-covid-19-ufc-252-rematch-with-magomed-ankalaev-off|access-date=2020-08-11|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco and Damon Martin|date=2020-08-11}} Cuțelaba tested positive yet again on the day of the event, resulting in another cancellation of this fight.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/08/ufc-on-espn-plus-33-ion-cutelaba-positive-covid-19-magomed-ankalaev-rematch-off|title=Ion Cutelaba tests positive for COVID-19, Magomed Ankalaev rematch off UFC on ESPN+ 33|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2020-08-29|access-date=2020-08-29}}\n\nA featherweight bout between [[Alex Caceres]] and [[Giga Chikadze]] was initially scheduled to take place three weeks earlier at [[UFC Fight Night: Lewis vs. Oleinik]], but was pushed back to this event due to undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/bruce-leeroy-treft-giga-chikadze-tijdens-ufc-evenement-op-8-augustus/|title=\"Bruce Leeroy\" meets Giga Chikadze at UFC event on August 8|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2020-07-17|access-date=2020-07-18}} {{in lang|nl}} In turn, Chikadze withdrew from the bout on August 26 after testing positive for COVID-19 and was briefly replaced by promotional newcomer [[Kevin Croom]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/alex-caceres-heeft-nieuwe-tegenstander-in-kevin-croom/|title=Alex Caceres has new opponent in Kevin COVID-19 test|publisher=mmafighting|author=Alexander K. Hannoun|date=2020-08-27}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Maki Pitolo]] and promotional newcomer [[Impa Kasanganay]] was briefly linked to [[UFC on ESPN: Munhoz vs. Edgar]] a week earlier.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/8/11/21364536/dana-white-maki-pitolo-to-welcome-dwcs-contract-winner-impa-kasanganay-at-ufc-event-on-aug-22|title=Maki Pitolo to welcome DWCS contract winner
