week (Aug. 10-16)|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2020-08-17|access-date=2020-08-18}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Saparbek Safarov]] and [[Julian Marquez]] was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/ufc-august-29-julian-marquez-saparbeg-safarov|title=Julian Marquez vs. Saparbeg Safarov joins UFC's Aug. 29 lineup|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-07-13|access-date=2020-08-24}} However, the bout was moved to [[UFC Fight Night: Blaydes vs. Lewis]] in November after Safarov faced travel restrictions related to the COVID-19 pandemic.{{Cite web|url=https://www.fightful.com/mma/report-julian-marquez-vs-saparbeg-safarov-moved-november-14-ufc-card#:~:text=A%20middleweight%20match%2Dup%20between,the%20November%2014%20UFC%20card.&text=The%20yet%20to%20be%20announced,main%20event%20to%20be%20announced.|title=Report: Julian Marquez vs. Saparbeg Safarov moved to November 14 UFC card|publisher=fightful.com|author=David Tees|date=2020-08-20|access-date=2020-08-24}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Hannah Cifers]] and Austin Springer missed weight for their respective bouts. Cifers weighed in at 117 pounds, one pound over the strawweight non-title fight limit. Springer weighed in at 151 pounds, five pounds over the featherweight non-title fight limit. Both of their bouts proceeded at catchweight and they were each fined 20% and 30% of their individual purses respectively, which went to their opponents [[Mallory Martin]] and [[Alex Caceres]].{{Cite Waterson vs. Hill]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Overeem vs. Sakai''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 176''''', '''''UFC on ESPN+ 34''''' and '''''UFC Vegas 9''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on September 5, 2020, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/29461257/sources-ufc-eyeing-alistair-overeem-vs-augusto-sakai-sept-5|title=Sources: UFC eyeing Overeem-Sakai for Sept. 5|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2020-07-14|access-date=2020-07-20}}\n\n== Background ==\nA heavyweight bout between the [[List of K-1 champions|2010 K-1 World Grand Prix Champion]], former [[List of Strikeforce champions#Heavyweight Championship|Strikeforce Heavyweight Champion]] and [[UFC Heavyweight Championship]] challenger [[Alistair Overeem]] and [[Augusto Sakai]] served as the event headliner.\n\nA women's bantamweight bout between former [[UFC Women's Flyweight Championship|UFC Women's Flyweight Champion]] [[Nicco Montaño]] and [[Julia Avila]] was originally scheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Lewis vs. Oleinik]].{{cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/nicco-montano-treft-julia-avila-na-drie-jaar-opnieuw-ditmaal-op-8-augustus-in-de-ufc/|title=Nicco Montaño will meet Julia Avila again after three years, this time on August 8 in the UFC|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2020-06-18|access-date=2020-06-18}} {{in lang|nl}} However, due to Montaño's coach John Wood testing positive for COVID-19, the bout was rescheduled for this event instead.{{cite web|url=https://www.sportskeeda.com/mma/news-nicco-montano-vs-julia-avila-delayed-coach-tests-positive|title=Nicco Montaño vs Julia Avila delayed after coach tests positive|publisher=sportskeeda.com|author=Paarth Pande|date=2020-07-12|access-date=2020-07-12}} In turn, it was announced on August 29 that Montaño herself tested positive for the disease and the bout was pushed back to [[UFC on ESPN: Holm vs. Aldana]] in October.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/8/29/21406871/nicco-montano-tests-positive-for-covid-19-rematch-with-julia-avila-moved-to-oct-3-ufc-event|title=Nicco Montano tests positive for COVID-19, rematch with Julia Avila moved to Oct. 3 UFC event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck and Damon Martin|date=2020-08-29|access-date=2020-08-29}}\n\nA women's flyweight bout between [[Maryna Moroz]] and [[Montana De La Rosa]] was scheduled for this event. However, Moroz was forced to withdraw as she was unable to secure a [[Travel visa|visa]] due to [[travel restrictions related to the COVID-19 pandemic]] and was replaced by [[Viviane Araújo (fighter)|Viviane Araújo]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/08/ufc-viviane-araujo-replaces-maryna-moroz-to-fight-montana-de-la-rosa-sept-5|title=Viviane Araujo replaces Maryna Moroz against Montana De La Rosa at Sept. 5 UFC Fight Night|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-08-11|access-date=2020-08-12}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Khama Worthy]] and [[Ottman Azaitar]] was previously scheduled to take place earlier this year at [[UFC 249]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/1/29/21113496/sijara-eubanks-vs-sarah-moras-khama-worthy-vs-ottman-azaitar-join-ufc-249-lineup|title=Khama Worthy vs. Ottman Azaitar join UFC 249 lineup|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Newswire|date=2020-01-29|access-date=2020-08-12}} However, the event was cancelled in early April due to the [[COVID-19 pandemic]].{{cite reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/worthy-vs-azaitar-en-simon-vs-kelleher-partijen-niet-tijdens-ufc-evenement-op-5-september/|title=Worthy vs. Azaitar and Simón vs. Kelleher not parties during briefly linked to this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/mayra-bueno-silva-treft-mara-romero-borella-tijdens-ufc-evenement-op-5-september/|title=Mayra Bueno Silva will face Mara Romero Borella at In turn, the pairing was targeted for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Ovince-St-Preux-Tests-Positive-for-COVID19-Bout-vs-Alonzo-Menifield-Postponed-to-Sept-5-174716|title=Ovince Saint Preux tests positive for COVID-19, bout vs. Alonzo Menifield postponed to Sept. 5|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2020-08-22|access-date=2020-08-24}}\n\nA women's bantamweight bout between [[Macy Chiasson]] and [[Sijara Eubanks]] was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/macy-chiasson-vs-sijara-eubanks-ingepland-voor-ufc-evenement-op-5-september/|title=Macy Chiasson vs. Sijara Eubanks scheduled for UFC event on September 5th|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2020-07-17|access-date=2020-07-18}} {{in lang|nl}} However, Chiasson pulled out of the fight for undisclosed medical reasons and was replaced by [[Karol Rosa]].{{Cite web|url=https://globoesporte.globo.com/combate/noticia/com-menos-de-duas-semanas-para-a-luta-karol-rosa-ganha-vaga-e-enfrenta-sijara-eubanks-no-ufc.ghtml|title=With less than two weeks to go, Karol Rosa wins a spot and faces Sijara Eubanks in the UFC|publisher=globoesporte.globo.com|author=Raphael Marinho|date=2020-08-26|access-date=2020-08-26}} {{in lang|pt}} Rosa pulled out on September 3 due to complications related to her weight cut. cancellations, the event took place with only seven bouts on the card, making it the smallest UFC event since [[The Ultimate Fighter 2 Finale]] in November 2005.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/29819488/late-cancellations-leave-ufc-fight-night-just-seven-bouts-smallest-card-2005|title=Late cancellations leave UFC Fight Night with just seven bouts and smallest card since 2005|work=espn.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2020-09-05|access-date=2020-09-05}}\n\n==Event==\nAll the fights were on the main card due to the many cancellations and changes. The card consisted of 7 mixed martial arts bouts, which were scheduled for three five-minute rounds, except for the main event fight between Overeem and Sakai, which would consist of five five-minute rounds. The fights were held behind closed doors due to the COVID-19 pandemic at the UFC Apex in Las Vegas, and the event was broadcast on ESPN+.\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card Arena and Forum|du Forum]] on [[Yas Island]], [[Abu Dhabi]], United Arab Emirates.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/UFC-253-Israel-AdesanyaPaulo-Costa-Title-Bout-Official-for-Sept-26-174602|title=UFC 253, Israel Adesanya-Paulo Costa title bout official for Sept. Okamoto|date=2020-09-04|access-date=2020-09-05}}\n\n== Background ==\nThe event was originally expected to take place on September 19, but it was moved to September 27. [[UFC Fight Night: Covington vs. Woodley]] took place on the former date.\n\nAlthough not officially announced by the organization, a [[UFC Lightweight Championship]] unification bout between current champion [[Khabib Nurmagomedov]] and interim champion [[Justin Gaethje]] (also former [[List of WSOF champions#Lightweight Championship|WSOF Lightweight Champion]]) was briefly linked to the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.essentiallysports.com/ufc-news-breaking-khabib-nurmagomedov-vs-justin-gaethje-set-to-scrap-at-ufc-253/|title=BREAKING: Khabib Nurmagomedov vs Justin Gaethje set to scrap at UFC 253|publisher=essentiallysports.com|author=Darryl Pinto|date=2020-06-10|access-date=2020-07-11}} However, the date did not materialize and their manager, Ali Abdelaziz, indicated that his clients would still face each other before the end of the year.{{Cite web|url=https://sports.yahoo.com/khabib-manager-insists-justin-gaethje-185309429.html|title=Khabib's manager insists Justin Gaethje bout will still happen in 2020|publisher=sports.yahoo.com|author=Staff|date=2020-07-22|access-date=2020-07-23}} The pairing was then scheduled for [[UFC 254]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/khabib-nurmagomedov-justin-gaethje-october-24-dana-white|title=Dana White announces Khabib Nurmagomedov vs. Justin Gaethje for Oct. 24|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Simon Head|date=2020-07-28|access-date=2020-07-28}}\n\n{{multiple image\n | align = left\n | total_width = 320\n | image1 = Israel Adesanya at UFC 230.jpg\n | width1 = 2300| height1 = 2900\n | alt1 =\n | caption1 =\n | image2 = Borrachinha galo.jpg\n | width2 = 2300| height2 = 2900\n | alt2 =\n | caption2 =\n | footer = With both Adesanya ''(left)'' and Costa ''(right)'' having no losses on their records (19–0 and 13–0, respectively), this bout represented just the second time in UFC history that two undefeated male athletes had met for a title. The only previous occasion was for the [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] over a decade ago, between former champions [[Rashad Evans]] and [[Lyoto Machida]] at [[UFC 98]] in 2009.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/08/ufc-253-israel-adesanya-paulo-costa-title-fight-of-the-year|title=Israel Adesanya: Paulo Costa title fight will be everything Yoel Romero should've been|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2020-08-18|access-date=2020-09-05}}\n}}\n\n[[Behind closed doors (sport)|Without fans in attendance]], the promotion did not have to worry about the local timing of the event, so it proceeded with normal timing for [[prime time]] hours on the [[Eastern Time Zone|east coast]] of North America. The main card began at 6:00 am (September 27) local time in Abu Dhabi, with a full preliminary card beginning at approximately 2:30 am [[Gulf Standard Time]].{{cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/ufc-fight-island-where-ufc-fight-island-fight-island-schedule-and-more|title=UFC Fight Island - Everything you need to know|publisher=ufc.com|author=Press Release|date=2020-09-17|access-date=2020-09-17}}\n\nA [[UFC Middleweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Israel Adesanya]] and [[Paulo Costa]] served as the event headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/israel-adesanya-vs-paulo-costa-middleweight-title-fight-ufc-253|title=Israel Adesanya vs. Paulo Costa middleweight title fight set for UFC 253|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2020-07-18|access-date=2020-07-21}} The bout was originally targeted for [[UFC 248]], but Costa was unable to compete on that card due to injury.{{Cite web|url=https://www.fightnewsaustralia.com/paulo-costa-injured-israel-adesanya-targets-yoel-romero-for-his-first-title-defence/|title=Paulo Costa injured, Israel Adesanya targets Yoel Romero for his first title defence|publisher=fightnewsaustralia.com|author=Katrina Mahoney|date=2019-10-30|access-date=2020-07-21}} In turn, Adesanya defended his title against former title challenger (as well as [[Wrestling at the 2000 Summer Olympics|2000 Olympic silver medalist]] and former world champion in [[freestyle wrestling]]) [[Yoel Romero]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/03/israel-adesanya-rips-paulo-costa-explains-ufc-248-fight-with-skidding-yoel-romero|title=Israel Adesanya rips Paulo Costa, explains UFC 248 fight with skidding Yoel Romero|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn and John Morgan|date=2020-03-05|access-date=2020-07-21}}\n\nA [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] bout for the vacant title between former title challenger [[Dominick Reyes]] and former [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#Light heavyweight championship|KSW Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[Jan Błachowicz]] served as the event's co-headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/dominick-reyes-jan-blachowicz-fight-undisputed-light-heavyweight-title-ufc-253|title=Reyes vs Blachowicz for light heavyweight title|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2020-08-18|access-date=2020-08-18}} Former two-time champion [[Jon Jones]] vacated the belt on August 17, citing issues with the UFC over salary negotiation and the desire to compete at heavyweight.{{cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/08/jon-jones-vacates-ufc-light-heavyweight-title-officially|title=Jon Jones vacates UFC title: this event.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/with-sergei-pavlovich-out-ciryl-gane-vs-shamil-abdurkhaimov-targeted-ufc-event-sept-19|title=With Sergei Pavlovich out, Ciryl Gane vs. web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/9/1/21410067/knee-injury-claudia-gadelha-out-ufc-253-fight-yan-xiaonan|title=Knee injury forces Claudia Gadelha out of UFC 253 fight with Yan bout between [[Jordan Espinosa]] and [[David Dvořák]] was expected to take place at this event, but was later rebooked for [[UFC Fight Night: Covington vs. Woodley]] due to undisclosed reasons.{{cite He will fight there on September 19|publisher=sportyzive.cz|author=Bohuslav Sedláček|date=2020-09-07|access-date=2020-09-11}} {{in lang|cs}} \n\nA women's bantamweight bout between [[Marion Reneau]] and [[Ketlen Vieira]] was scheduled for the event. However, Reneau was pulled from the bout on September 16 citing an undisclosed injury and replaced by [[Sijara Eubanks]].{{cite web|url=http://mma.uno/tras-baja-de-marion-reneau-ketlen-vieira-tiene-nueva-oponente-para-el-ufc-253/|title=After Marion Reneau's leave, Ketlen Vieira has a new opponent for UFC 253|publisher=mma.uno|author=Julio Fernando Navas|date=2020-09-16|access-date=2020-09-17}} {{in lang|es}}\n\n[[Nate Landwehr]] was expected to face [[Shane Young]] in a featherweight bout at this event. However, Landwehr was pulled from the event after he [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]] and replaced by promotional newcomer [[Ľudovít Klein]].{{cite web|url=https://sport.sme.sk/c/22490643/ludovit-klein-si-predstavi-v-ufc.html|title=A historic moment. Slovakia will have a wrestler in the prestigious UFC|publisher=sport.sme.sk|author=Miroslav Kováčik|date=2020-09-17|access-date=2020-09-17}} {{in lang|sk}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Casey Kenney]] and [[Heili Alateng]] was originally expected to take place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/casey-kenney-vs-heili-alateng-added-to-ufc-253-lineup|title=Casey Kenney vs. Heili Alateng added to UFC 253 lineup|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-07-19|access-date=2020-07-20}} However, it was moved to [[UFC on ESPN: Holm vs. Aldana]] a week later due to undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://www.scmp.com/sport/martial-arts/mixed-martial-arts/article/3100443/ufc-chinas-mongolian-knight-heili-alateng|title=UFC: China's 'Mongolian Knight' Heili Alateng looking to carve up bantamweight division|publisher=scmp.com|author=Matthew Scott|date=2020-09-07|access-date=2020-09-19}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Zubaira Tukhugov]] and [[Ľudovít Klein]] missed weight for their respective featherweight bouts. Both fighters weighed in at 150 pounds, four pounds over the non-title fight limit. Both of their bouts proceeded at a catchweight and they were each fined a percentage of their individual purses, which went to their opponents [[Hakeem Dawodu]] and [[Shane Young]].{{Cite arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on September 19, 2020, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://empiresportsmedia.com/mma/ufc-claudia-gadelha-meets-yan-xiaonan-on-september-26th/|title=UFC: Claudia Gadelha meets Yan Xiaonan on September 26th|publisher=empiresportsmedia.com|author=Daniel Cunningham|date=2020-07-07|access-date=2020-07-10}}\n\n== Background ==\nThe event was originally expected to take place on September 26, but it was moved up a week. [[UFC 253]] took place on the former date.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/UFC-253-Israel-AdesanyaPaulo-Costa-Title-Bout-Official-for-Sept-26-174602|title=UFC 253, Israel Adesanya-Paulo Costa title bout official for Sept. 26|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2020-08-15|access-date=2020-08-16}}\n\nUFC officials had initially targeted a welterweight bout between former [[UFC Welterweight Championship|UFC Welterweight Champion]] [[Tyron Woodley]] and former interim champion [[Colby Covington]] to serve as the event headliner for [[UFC on ESPN: Munhoz vs. Edgar]], but Woodley announced that the date did not provide him with time suitable enough to prepare because of injuries incurred during his most recent fight.{{Cite web|url=https://abcnews.go.com/Sports/tyron-woodley-open-colby-covington-fight-august/story?id=71826288|title=Tyron Woodley open to Colby Covington fight, but not in August|publisher=abcnews.go.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2020-07-16|access-date=2020-07-27}} The pairing headlined this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.essentiallysports.com/ufc-news-tyron-woodley-reveals-date-of-his-clash-against-colby-covington-september-19/|title=Tyron Woodley reveals date of his clash against Colby Covington|publisher=essentiallysports.com|author=Reubyn Coutinho|date=2020-08-15|access-date=2020-08-16}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Ciryl Gane]] and [[Shamil Abdurakhimov]] was expected to take place at this event.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmanews.com/shamil-abdurakhimov-vs-ciryl-gane-announced-for-sep-19/|title=Shamil Abdurakhimov vs. Ciryl Gane announced for Sep. 19|publisher=mmanews.com|author=Clyde Aidoo|date=2020-08-25|access-date=2020-09-04}} The pairing was originally scheduled for [[UFC 249]], but Gane was forced to pull out of the event after he was struck by a [[pneumothorax]] in training.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/3/5/21166938/ciryl-gane-out-ufc-249-fight-shamil-abdurakhimov|title=Ciryl Gane out of UFC 249 fight with Shamil Abdurakhimov|date=2020-03-05|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|access-date=2020-07-15}} Subsequently, the pairing was rescheduled for [[UFC 251]] and cancelled a second time as Abdurakhimov was removed from the bout for undisclosed reasons.{{cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/sergei-pavlovich-treft-ciryl-gane-tijdens-8-augustus-evenement/|title=Sergei Pavlovich encounters Ciryl Gane during the August 8 event|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2020-04-14|access-date=2020-07-15}} {{in lang|nl}} They were later rebooked for [[UFC 253]], before eventually having the bout shifted to this event.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/with-sergei-pavlovich-out-ciryl-gane-vs-shamil-abdurkhaimov-targeted-ufc-event-sept-19|title=With Sergei Pavlovich out, Ciryl Gane vs. Shamil Abdurakhimov targeted for Sept. 19 UFC event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-07-15|access-date=2020-07-15}} In turn, the bout then shifted again for undisclosed reasons and was expected to take place four weeks later at [[UFC Fight Night: Ortega vs. The Korean Zombie]].{{cite web|url=https://boxemag.com/ufc/ufc-france/item/cyril-gane-vs-shamil-abdurakhimov-rebooke-pour-le-19-septembre.html|title=Cyril Gane vs Shamil Abdurakhimov rebooked for October 17|publisher=boxemag.com|author=Staff|date=2020-09-09|access-date=2020-09-09}} {{in lang|fr}}\n\nA flyweight bout between [[Jordan Espinosa]] and [[David Dvořák]] was expected to take place at [[UFC 253]], but was later rebooked for this event due to undisclosed reasons.{{cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/flyweights-jordan-espinosa-en-david-dvorak-toegevoegd-aan-ufc-253/|title=Flyweights Jordan However, Gall pulled out on September 11 due to an injury.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/9/11/21433495/mickey-gall-injured-out-of-fight-with-miguel-baeza-at-ufc-vegas-11|title=Mickey Gall injured, out of fight with Miguel Baeza at UFC Vegas 11|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2020-09-11|access-date=2020-09-12}} He was replaced by Jeremiah Wells.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/09/ufc-on-espn-plus-36-miguel-baeza-jeremiah-wells-replacement|title=Miguel Baeza now fights Jeremiah Wells at UFC on ESPN+ 36|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Dave Doyle|date=2020-09-15|access-date=2020-09-15}} On September 17, it was announced that the bout was cancelled due to undisclosed reasons.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/09/ufc-on-espn-plus-36-miguel-baeza-vs-jeremiah-wells-removed|title=Miguel Baeza vs. Jeremiah Wells removed from UFC on ESPN+ 36|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2020-09-17|access-date=2020-09-17}}\n\nA featherweight bout between [[Mirsad Bektić]] and [[Luiz Eduardo Garagorri]] was scheduled for the event. He was fined $8,500 and before he is re-licensed in Las Vegas, Price will also have to pay a prosecution fee of $145.36.\n\n==See also==\n{{Portal|Martial Arts|United States}}\n* [[List of UFC Holm vs. Aldana''''' (also known as '''''UFC on ESPN 16''''' and '''''UFC Fight Island 4''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on October 4, 2020 at the [[Yas Island#du Arena and Forum|du Forum]] on [[Yas Island]], [[Abu Dhabi]], United Arab Emirates.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/9/4/21423733/ufc-253-kicks-off-fight-island-return-abu-dhabi-september-26|title=UFC 253 kicks off Fight Island return in Abu Okamoto|date=2020-09-04|access-date=2020-09-05}}\n\n== Background ==\n[[Behind closed doors (sport)|Without fans in attendance]], the promotion did not have to worry about the local timing of the event, so the plan was to proceed with normal timing for [[prime time]] hours on the [[Eastern Time Zone|east coast]] of North America. The main card was scheduled to begin at 6:30 am (October 4) local time in Abu Dhabi, with a full preliminary card beginning at approximately 3:30 am [[Gulf Standard Time]].{{cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/ufc-fight-island-where-ufc-fight-island-fight-island-schedule-and-more|title=UFC Fight Island - Everything you need to know|publisher=ufc.com|author=Press Release|date=2020-09-17|access-date=2020-09-17}}\n\nA women's bantamweight bout between former [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship|UFC Women's Bantamweight Champion]] [[Holly Holm]] and [[Irene Aldana]] served as the event headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://www.fightful.com/mma/irene-aldana-tests-positive-coronavirus-bout-holly-holm-reportedly-rescheduled-october|title=Irene Aldana tests positive for coronavirus, bout with Holly Holm reportedly rescheduled for October|author=David Tees|publisher=fightful.com|date=2020-07-22|access-date=2020-07-29}} The pairing was originally scheduled to headline [[UFC Fight Night: Brunson vs. Shahbazyan]]. However, it was reported on July 22 that Aldana had pulled out of the fight due to testing positive for COVID-19.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/7/22/21334843/irene-aldana-covid-19-positive-scratched-fight-with-holly-holm-at-aug-1-ufc-event|title=Irene Aldana: COVID-19 positive scratched fight with Holly Holm at Aug. 1 UFC event|author=Steven Marrocco|publisher=mmafighting.com|date=2020-07-22|access-date=2020-07-22}} Holm was also removed from the card as well, with the pairing left intact and ultimately rescheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/ufc-on-espn-plus-31-brunson-vs-shahbazyan-to-headline-holm-vs-aldana-postponed|title=Brunson vs. Shahbazyan promoted to UFC on ESPN+ 31 headliner after Holm vs. Aldana postponed|author=Danny Segura|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|date=2020-07-22|access-date=2020-07-22}} \n\nBen Sosoli was briefly linked to a heavyweight bout with [[Yorgan De Castro]]. However, Sosoli pulled out of the fight in late July citing an eye surgery and was replaced by [[Carlos Felipe]].{{Cite web|url=https://globoesporte.globo.com/combate/noticia/ultimate-confirma-duelo-entre-carlos-boi-e-yorgan-de-castro-para-evento-em-outubro.ghtml|title=UFC confirms fight between Carlos Boi and Yorgan De Castro for event in October|author=Raphael Marinho and Zeca Azevedo|date=2020-07-30|publisher=globoesporte.globo.com|access-date=2020-07-30}} {{in lang|pt}}\n\nA women's bantamweight bout between former [[UFC Women's Flyweight Championship|UFC Women's Flyweight Champion]] [[Nicco Montaño]] and [[Julia Avila]] was originally scheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Lewis vs. Oleinik]].{{cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/nicco-montano-treft-julia-avila-na-drie-jaar-opnieuw-ditmaal-op-8-augustus-in-de-ufc/|title=Nicco Montaño will meet Julia Avila again after three years, this time on August 8 in the UFC|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2020-06-18|access-date=2020-06-18}} {{in lang|nl}} However, due to Montaño’s coach John Wood testing positive for COVID-19, the bout was rescheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Overeem vs. Sakai]].{{Cite web|title=Nicco Montano vs. Julia Avila matchup moved to UFC event on Sept. 5 following coach’s positive COVID-19 test|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/7/11/21321100/nicco-montano-vs-julia-avila-matchup-moved-to-ufc-sept-5-following-coachs-positive-covid-19-test|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2020-07-11|access-date=2020-08-30}} In turn, it was announced on August 29 that Montaño herself tested positive for [[Coronavirus disease 2019|the disease]] and the bout was once again pushed back to this event.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/8/29/21406871/nicco-montano-tests-positive-for-covid-19-rematch-with-julia-avila-moved-to-oct-3-ufc-event|title=Nicco Montano tests positive for COVID-19, rematch with Julia Avila moved to Oct. 3 UFC event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck and Damon Martin|date=2020-08-29|access-date=2020-08-29}} On September 3, it was announced that Montaño withdrew from the bout due to travel restrictions. In turn, Avila was rescheduled to face [[Sijara Eubanks]] at [[UFC Fight Night: Waterson vs. Hill]] on September 12.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/09/ufc-news-sijara-eubanks-loses-karol-rosa-fight-meets-julia-avila-sept-12|title=Sijara Eubanks loses UFC on ESPN+ 34 bout vs. Karol Rosa, now meets Julia Avila on Sept. 12|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2020-09-03|access-date=2020-09-03}}{{Cite at UFC event on Sept. 12|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck and Guilherme Cruz|date=2020-09-03|access-date=2020-09-04}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] challengers [[Thiago Santos]] and [[Glover Teixeira]] was initially scheduled to headline [[UFC Fight Night: Waterson vs. Hill]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/thiago-santos-vs-glover-teixeira-light-heavyweight-ufc-main-event-sept-12|title=Thiago Santos vs. Glover Teixeira in the works to headline UFC's Sept. 12 for COVID-19]] a week prior to the fight.{{Cite web|title=Glover Teixeira tests positive for COVID-19, fight against Thiago Santos rescheduled for UFC event on Oct. MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://readmma.com/2020/07/02/roxanne-modafferi-vs-andrea-lee-ii-slated-for-september/|title=Roxanne Modafferi vs. Andrea Lee II slated for September|publisher=readmma.com|author=Graeme Harper|date=2020-07-02|access-date=2020-07-10}}\n\n== Background ==\nA light heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] challengers [[Thiago Santos]] and [[Glover Teixeira]] was originally scheduled to serve as the event headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/thiago-santos-vs-glover-teixeira-light-heavyweight-ufc-main-event-sept-12|title=Thiago Santos vs. Glover Teixeira in the works to headline UFC's Sept. 12 event on Oct. 3|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/9/3/21419531/glover-teixeira-tests-positive-COVID-19-fight-against-thiago-santos-rescheduled-ufc-event-on-oct-3|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2020-09-03|access-date=2020-09-03}}\n\nA women's strawweight bout between former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Atomweight Championship|Invicta FC Atomweight Champion]] [[Michelle Waterson]] and former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Strawweight Championship|Invicta FC Strawweight Champion]] [[Angela Hill]] was initially scheduled to take place three weeks earlier at [[UFC on ESPN: Munhoz vs. Edgar]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/06/angela-hil-michelle-waterson-strawweight-ufc-august-22|title=Angela Hill meets Michelle Waterson on UFC's Aug. 22 web|url=https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2020/8/13/21366719/ufc-report-michelle-waterson-angela-hill-fight-night-vegas-womens-mma-news|title=Report: Michelle Waterson vs. Angela Hill off UFC Vegas 7, Subsequently, the pairing was announced as the new main event after the postponement of Santos/Teixeira.{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/29808629/sources-michelle-waterson-vs-angela-hill-new-headliner-sept-12|title=Michelle Waterson vs. Angela Hill new headliner on Sept. Teixeira-Santos moved|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-09-04|access-date=2020-09-04}} This was the first time in UFC history that a bout involving an [[African Americans|African-American woman]] served as the event headliner.{{cite web|author=Danny Segura and John Morgan|date=2020-09-10|title=On verge of history, Angela Hill proud to be first lightweight bout between [[Roosevelt Roberts]] and [[Matt Frevola]] was previously scheduled to take place [[Canceled UFC event on April 25, 2020|at another event earlier in the year]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/02/matt-frevola-vs-roosevelt-roberts-added-to-ufc-on-espn-31|title=Matt Frevola vs. Roosevelt Roberts added to UFC on ESPN+ web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/4/9/21215721/ufc-249-upcoming-events-cancelled-due-to-coronavirus-dana-white-promises-fight-island-will-proceed|title=UFC 249, upcoming events postponed due to [[Kevin Croom]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/9/11/21432308/matt-frevola-out-kevin-croom-steps-in-to-fight-roosevelt-roberts-at-ufc-vegas-10|title=Matt Frevola out, Kevin Croom steps in to fight Roosevelt Roberts at UFC Vegas 10|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Newswire|date=2020-09-11|access-date=2020-09-11}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between [[Ed Herman]] and [[Gerald Meerschaert]] was previously scheduled for August 1 at [[UFC Fight Night: Brunson vs. Shahbazyan]].{{cite Allan already had a bout booked against [[Roman Dolidze]] on November 21 at [[UFC 255]], but was expected to compete in both of them.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/08/john-allan-replaces-gerald-meerschaert-simultaneously-booked-ufc-255|title=John Allan replaces Gerald Meerschaert at UFC on ESPN+ 35, still keeps UFC 255 booking|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2020-08-20|access-date=2020-08-20}} Herman then suffered another opponent change on September 3, as Allan pulled out due to [[Visa (document)|visa]] issues and was replaced by [[Mike Rodríguez (fighter)|Mike Rodríguez]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/9/3/21420113/ed-herman-to-fight-mike-rodriguez-at-sept-12-ufc-event|title=Ed Herman to fight Mike Rodriguez at Sept. 12 UFC event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee, Steven Marrocco, and Damon Martin|date=2020-09-03|access-date=2020-09-03}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Khama Worthy]] and [[Ottman Azaitar]] was previously scheduled to take place earlier this year at [[UFC 249]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/1/29/21113496/sijara-eubanks-vs-sarah-moras-khama-worthy-vs-ottman-azaitar-join-ufc-249-lineup|title=Khama Worthy vs. Ottman expected to take place at this event.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/7/18/21329075/tagir-ulanbekov-vs-bruno-silva-slated-for-ufc-event-on-sept-12|title=Tagir Ulanbekov vs. Bruno Silva During the second round, Rodríguez landed a knee to Herman's body in a clinch up against the cage. Herman immediately dropped and before Rodríguez was able to deliver more strikes, referee Chris Tognoni stepped in and called a pause to the action due to a possible low blow. Herman was allowed five minutes to recover as a standard procedure and eventually won the fight in the third round. Also of note in the appeal, Rodríguez's team alleged that during the finishing sequence Herman illegally had his toes in the fence to sweep into top position and gain more leverage for the hold.{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/29916666/mike-rodriguez-appeals-loss-ed-herman-ref-low-blow-ruling|title=Mike Rodriguez appeals loss to Ed Herman over ref's low-blow ruling|work=espn.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2020-09-18|access-date=2020-09-19}} Subsequently, the request for an appeal was denied by the [[Nevada State Athletic Commission]] (NSAC).{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/11/ufc-news-tyson-chartier-pushes-mike-rodriguez-appeal|title=NAC rejects Mike Rodriguez's appeal of UFC loss|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun and Nolan King|date=2020-11-23|access-date=2020-11-23}}\n\nOn November 4, it was announced that the NSAC issued a four and a half month suspension for Kevin Croom, after he tested positive for [[marijuana]] in a pre-fight drug test. They also announced that Croom's victory was overturned to a no contest due to the violation. He was fined $1,800 and before he is re-licensed in Las Vegas, Croom will also have to pay a prosecution fee of $145.36.\n\n== See also ==\n{{Portal|Martial Arts|United States}}\n* [[List of UFC November|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=2020-10-07|access-date=2020-10-07}}\n\n== Background ==\nA [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Deiveson Figueiredo]] and former [[UFC Bantamweight Championship|UFC Bantamweight Champion]] [[Cody Garbrandt]] was expected to serve as the event headliner. the event.{{Cite web|url=https://globoesporte.globo.com/combate/noticia/cody-garbrandt-deixa-luta-e-deiveson-figueiredo-vai-enfrentar-alex-perez-pelo-cinturao.ghtml|title=Cody Garbrandt pulls out and took place on October 24, 2020 at the [[Yas Island#du Arena and Forum|du Forum]] on [[Yas Island]], [[Abu Dhabi]], United Arab Emirates.{{Cite web|url=https://www.cnn.com/2020/07/28/sport/khabib-nurmagomedov-gaethje-ufc-white-spt-intl/index.html|title=Khabib Nurmagomedov to fight Justin Gaethje in October return, says UFC president Dana White|publisher=cnn.com|author=Don Riddell, Glen Levy and Ben Church|date=2020-07-28|access-date=2020-07-28}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/9/4/21423733/ufc-253-kicks-off-fight-island-return-abu-dhabi-september-26|title=UFC 253 kicks off Fight Island return in Abu Dhabi|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2020-09-04|access-date=2020-09-05}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/ufc/story/_/id/29814358/ufc-253-254-highlight-five-events-fight-island-return|title=Sources: UFC 253, 254 to be held at Fight Island|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2020-09-04|access-date=2020-09-05}}\n\n== Background ==\nThe event proceeded with local timing for [[prime time]] hours. The main card began at 10:00 pm (October 24) local time in Abu Dhabi, with a full preliminary card beginning at approximately 6:15 pm [[Gulf Standard Time]].{{cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/ufc-fight-island-where-ufc-fight-island-fight-island-schedule-and-more|title=UFC Fight Island - Everything you need to know|publisher=ufc.com|author=Press Release|date=2020-09-17|access-date=2020-09-17}}\n\nA [[UFC Lightweight Championship]] unification bout between current champion [[Khabib Nurmagomedov]] and interim champion [[Justin Gaethje]] (also former [[WSOF Lightweight Championship|WSOF Lightweight Champion]]) served as the event headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/khabib-nurmagomedov-justin-gaethje-october-24-dana-white|title=Dana White announces Khabib Nurmagomedov vs. Justin Gaethje for Oct. 24|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Simon Head|date=2020-07-28|access-date=2020-07-28}} The bout was briefly linked to [[UFC 253]], but the date did not materialize and instead took place four weeks later.{{Cite web|url=https://sports.yahoo.com/khabib-manager-insists-justin-gaethje-185309429.html|title=Khabib's manager insists Justin Gaethje bout will still happen in 2020|publisher=sports.yahoo.com|author=Staff|date=2020-07-22|access-date=2020-07-28}} On September 17, the UFC announced the signing of former three-time [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Lightweight World Championship|Bellator Lightweight World Champion]] [[Michael Chandler]], who served as backup and a potential replacement for this fight.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/09/michael-chandler-signs-with-ufc-backup-khabib-nurmagomedov-justin-gaethje-ufc-254-bellator|title=Michael Chandler signs with UFC, will serve as backup for Khabib-Gaethje at UFC 254|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2020-09-17|access-date=2020-09-17}}\n\n[[File:Хабиб Нурмагомедов-2. 12.9.2019.jpg|thumb|left|210px|[[Khabib Nurmagomedov]] held a perfect 28–0 record leading into the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2020/10/23/21530844/ufc-254-main-card-predictions-preview-and-analysis|title=UFC 254 predictions, preview, and analysis|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Ryan Harkness|date=2020-10-23|access-date=2020-10-24}}]]\n\nA middleweight bout between former [[UFC Middleweight Championship|UFC Middleweight Champion]] [[Robert Whittaker (fighter)|Robert Whittaker]] and [[Jared Cannonier]] was initially scheduled to take place at [[UFC 248]] in March. However, Whittaker pulled out for personal reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/01/ufc-248-robert-whittaker-out-jared-cannonieir-fight-report|title=Robert Whittaker out of UFC 248 fight against Jared Cannonier|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2020-01-15|access-date=2020-08-01}} The pairing was rescheduled for this event.{{Cite web|title=Ex-champ Robert Whittaker meets Jared Cannonier at UFC 254|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/08/robert-whittaker-jared-cannonier-middleweight-ufc-254-october|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2020-08-01|access-date=2020-08-01}}\n\nDespite never being officially announced, a featherweight bout between [[The Ultimate Fighter: Latin America]] featherweight winner [[Yair Rodríguez]] and [[Zabit Magomedsharipov]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite news|url=https://www.bjpenn.com/mma-news/ufc/report-zabit-magomedsharipov-vs-yair-rodriguez-rescheduled-for-ufc-254/|title=Zabit Magomedsharipov vs. Yair Rodriguez rescheduled for UFC 254|publisher=bjpenn.com|author=Cole Shelton|date=2020-08-14|access-date=2020-08-15}} The pairing had been scheduled on two previous occasions, first at [[UFC 228]] in September 2018 and then at [[UFC Fight Night: Smith vs. Rakić]] in August. However, Rodríguez pulled out of both events with injury.{{Cite news|url=https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2018/8/23/17773928/injury-yair-rodriguez-ufc-228-fight-zabit-magomedsharipov-mma-news|title=Injury forces Yair Rodriguez out of UFC 228 fight vs. Zabit Magomedsharipov|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Harry Davies|date=2018-08-23|access-date=2020-09-05|archive-date=2020-11-09|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20201109010531/https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2018/8/23/17773928/injury-yair-rodriguez-ufc-228-fight-zabit-magomedsharipov-mma-news|url-status=dead}}{{Cite web|title=Dana White confirms Yair Rodriguez injured, out of fight vs. Zabit Magomedsharipov|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/08/dana-white-confirms-yair-rodriguez-injured-out-of-fight-vs-zabit-magomedsharipov|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2020-08-04|access-date=2020-09-05}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/8/10/21362152/zabit-magomedsharipov-announces-he-will-not-fight-on-aug-29-returning-to-russia-for-now|title=Zabit Magomedsharipov announces he will not fight on Aug. 29, returning to Russia for now|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2020-08-10|access-date=2020-09-05}}\n\n[[Magomed Ankalaev]] and [[Ion Cuțelaba]] first met in a light heavyweight bout at [[UFC Fight Night: Benavidez vs. Figueiredo]], where Ankalaev won via controversial knockout.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/2/29/21159660/ufc-norfolk-results-magomed-ankalaev-earns-ko-over-ion-cutelaba-in-highly-controversial-stoppage|title=UFC Norfolk results: Magomed Ankalaev earns KO over Ion Cutelaba in highly controversial stoppage|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2020-02-29|access-date=2020-07-12}} Due to the controversy of the stoppage, the UFC booked the rematch for [[UFC 249]], on its original April 18 date.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/3/5/21167016/ion-cutelaba-vs-magomed-ankalaev-rebooked-for-ufc-249-following-controversial-stoppage|title=Ion Cutelaba vs. Magomed Ankalaev re-booked for UFC 249 following controversial stoppage|author=Damon Martin|publisher=mmafighting.com|date=2020-03-05|access-date=2020-07-12}} However, Ankalaev was forced to pull out of the event due to [[travel restrictions related to the COVID-19 pandemic]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/4/6/21209211/ufc-announces-updated-lineup-for-ufc-249-andrade-vs-namajunas-2-still-co-headlines|title=UFC 249 fight card announced, Andrade vs. Namajunas 2 still co-headlines|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2020-04-06|access-date=2020-04-07}} They were later rebooked for [[UFC 252]], but Cuțelaba then pulled out after testing positive for COVID-19 and the bout was rescheduled for UFC Fight Night: Smith vs. Rakić.{{Cite web|title=Ion Cuțelaba tests positive for COVID-19, rematch against Magomed Ankalaev bumped from UFC 252 to August 29 event|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/8/11/21364211/ion-cutelaba-positive-for-covid-19-ufc-252-rematch-with-magomed-ankalaev-off|access-date=2020-08-11|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco and Damon Martin|date=2020-08-11}} Cuțelaba tested positive yet again on the day of the event, resulting in another cancellation of this fight.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/08/ufc-on-espn-plus-33-ion-cutelaba-positive-covid-19-magomed-ankalaev-rematch-off|title=Ion Cutelaba tests positive for COVID-19, Magomed Ankalaev rematch off UFC on ESPN+ 33|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2020-08-29|access-date=2020-08-29}} In turn, they were rescheduled for a third time and met at this event.{{cite web|url=https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2020/9/9/21429758/ufc-254-khabib-vs-gaethje-fight-booking-ion-cutelaba-vs-magomed-ankalaev-rematch-covid-mma|title=Fifth time's a charm - UFC re-books Cutelaba vs. Ankalaev again|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Zane Promotional newcomer Isi Fitikefu weighed in as an alternate for this bout.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/10/ufc-254-khabib-nurmagomedov-justin-gaethje-weigh-in-results-video-stream|title=UFC 254 weigh-in results: Khabib needs the towel, but makes weight for title fight|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2020-10-23|access-date=2020-10-23}}\n\n[[Elizeu Zaleski dos Santos]] was expected to face [[Shavkat Rakhmonov]] in a welterweight bout at the event.{{cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/elizeu-zaleski-dos-santos-vs-shavkat-rakhmonov-toegevoegd-aan-ufc-254-in-abu-dhabi/|title=Elizeu Zaleski dos Santos vs. Shavkat Rakhmonov added to UFC 254 in Abu Dhabi|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2020-09-11|access-date=2020-09-11}} {{in lang|nl}} However, dos Santos pulled out in late September due to a knee injury that would require surgery.{{cite web|url=https://agfight.com/noticias/elizeu-capoeira-rompe-o-ligamento-do-joelho-e-esta-fora-do-ufc-254/|title=Elizeu 'Capoeira' injuries his knee ligament and is out of UFC 254|publisher=agfight.com|author=Diego Ribas and Natássia del Fischer|date=2020-09-30|access-date=2020-09-30}} {{in lang|pt}} He was replaced by [[Alex Oliveira]].{{cite web|url=https://www.uol.com.br/esporte/ultimas-noticias/ag-fight/2020/10/07/alex-cowboy-substitui-elizeu-capoeira-e-encara-rival-invicto-no-ufc-254.htm|title=Alex 'Cowboy' replaces Elizeu 'Capoeira' and faces undefeated rival at UFC 254|publisher=uol.com.br|author=Natássia del Fischer|date=2020-10-07|access-date=2020-10-07}} {{in lang|pt}}\n\nA lightweight bout between former champion [[Rafael dos Anjos]] and [[Islam Makhachev]] was expected to take place at the event.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/08/rafael-dos-anjos-returns-to-lightweight-islam-makhachev-ufc-254|title=Rafael dos Anjos returns to lightweight, meets Islam Makhachev at UFC 254|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun and Mike Bohn|date=2020-08-20|access-date=2020-10-08}} However, it was announced on October 8 that dos Anjos [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]] and was pulled from the bout.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/10/8/21508573/rafael-dos-anjos-announces-covid-19-positive-scratch-from-ufc-254|title=Rafael dos Anjos announces COVID-19 positive, scratch from UFC 254|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Newswire|date=2020-10-08|access-date=2020-10-08}} A replacement could not be found and Makhachev was also removed from the event.{{cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Islam-Makhachev-Wont-Receive-Replacement-Opponent-at-UFC-254-175648|title=Islam Makhachev won't receive replacement opponent at UFC 254|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2020-10-14|access-date=2020-10-14}} The pairing was left intact and rescheduled for November 14 at [[UFC Fight Night: Felder vs. dos Anjos]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/breaking-islam-makhachev-vs-rafael-dos-anjos-opnieuw-ingepland-ditmaal-voor-14-november/|title=Islam Makhachev vs. Rafael Dos Anjos rescheduled, this time for November 14th|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2020-10-20|access-date=2020-10-20}} {{in lang|nl}}\n\n[[Cynthia Calvillo]] and former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Bantamweight Championship|Invicta FC Bantamweight Champion]] [[Lauren Murphy]] were scheduled to meet in a women's flyweight bout at the event.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/8/13/21368207/cynthia-calvillo-vs-lauren-murphy-in-the-works-for-ufc-254|title=Cynthia Calvillo vs. Lauren Murphy in the works for UFC 254|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Jose Youngs and Mike Heck|date=2020-08-13|access-date=2020-10-16}} However, Calvillo withdrew from the bout on October 15 after testing positive for COVID-19 and was replaced by promotional newcomer Liliya Shakirova.{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/30123027/cynthia-calvillo-withdraws-ufc-254-bout-lauren-murphy-replaced-liliya-shakirova|title=Cynthia Calvillo withdraws from UFC 254 bout with Lauren Murphy, replaced by Liliya Shakirova|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2020-10-15|access-date=2020-10-16}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Umar Nurmagomedov]] and [[Sergey Morozov (fighter)|Sergey Morozov]] was expected to take place at the event. However, it was announced on October 18 that Nurmagomedov had been hospitalized due to an illness and the bout was scrapped.{{cite web|url= https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/10/ufc-254-umar-nurmagomedov-ruled-out-octagon-debut|title=Umar Nurmagomedov hospitalized in Dubai, ruled out of octagon debut at UFC 254|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Simon Head|date=2020-10-18|access-date=2020-10-18}} Morozov remained as an alternate for the catchweight bout of 140 pounds between [[Nathaniel Wood (fighter)|Nathaniel Wood]] and [[Casey Kenney]].{{Cite web|url=https://fansided.com/2020/10/23/ufc-254-bantamweight-prospect-sergey-morozov/|title=UFC 254: Who is bantamweight prospect Sergey Morozov?|publisher=fansided.com|author=Zain Bando|date=2020-10-23|access-date=2020-10-23}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Alex Oliveira]] and [[Joel Álvarez]] missed weight for their respective bouts. Oliveira weighed in at 173 pounds, two pounds over the welterweight non-title fight limit. Álvarez weighed in at 159.5 pounds, three and a half pounds over the lightweight non-title fight limit. Both of their bouts proceeded at catchweight and they were each fined 20% and 30% of their individual purses respectively, which went to their opponents Shavkat Rakhmonov and [[Alexander Yakovlev (fighter)|Alexander Yakovlev]].\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Khabib Nurmagomedov]] (c)\n|def.\n|[[Justin Gaethje]] (ic)\n|Technical web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-254-bonuses-khabib-nurmagomedov-performance-of-the-night|title=UFC 254 bonuses: Khabib earns 'Performance of the Night' in final fight|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Dave Doyle|date=2020-10-24|access-date=2020-10-24}}\n*'''Fight of the Night: Casey Kenney vs. Nathaniel Wood'''\n*'''Performance of the Night: Khabib Nurmagomedov''' and '''Magomed Ankalaev'''\n\n==Aftermath==\nIn Khabib Nurmagomedov’s post-fight interview, he announced his retirement from mixed martial arts and explained that he had promised his mother that he would not continue to fight without his late father (who died from complications related to an existing heart condition while positive with COVID-19 in July). \"Today I want to say it was my last fight. No way I'm gonna come here without my father,\" Nurmagomedov said. \"It was first time after what happened with my father. When UFC called me with Justin, I talked with my mother three days. She don't (want me) to go fight without father, but I promised her it's gonna be my last fight. And if I give my word, I have to follow this.\"{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/10/ufc-254-khabib-nurmagomedov-dominates-announces-retirement-ufc-254-justin-gaethje|title=Khabib Nurmagomedov dominates Justin Gaethje, retires after UFC 254 submission win|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Dave Doyle|date=2020-10-24|access-date=2020-10-24}}\n\n==Viewership==\nJust three days prior to fight night, UFC officials said they had tracked media coverage for the card in more than 50 countries, totaling more than 23 million impressions.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/10/ufc-254-dana-white-reveals-trending-numbers-khabib-nurmagomedov-justin-gaethje|title=Dana White reveals trending numbers that qualify Khabib vs. Gaethje as UFC blockbuster|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=John Morgan|date=2020-10-21|access-date=2020-11-10}}\n\nIn Russia, UFC 254 was aired on [[REN TV]], where it drew more than 10.8 million viewers on live television, ranking first place in [[Television in Russia|Russian television]] ratings. The event was watched by 19.2% of the 25-54 age demographic in Russia, including 25.7% of males in that demographic. In the capital city [[Moscow]], the event was watched by 19.8% of the 25-54 age demographic, including 26.8% of males in that demographic.{{cite news |title=Бой Нурмагомедова с Гэтжи принес РЕН ТВ первое место в рейтинге |trans-title=Nurmagomedov's fight with Gaethje brought REN TV first place in the rankings |url=https://ren.tv/news/v-rossii/764886-boi-nurmagomedova-s-getzhi-prines-ren-tv-pervoe-mesto-v-reitinge |access-date=9 November 2020 |publisher=[[REN TV]] |date=26 October 2020 |language=ru}}\n\n== 22|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Jesse Holland|date=2020-06-30|access-date=2020-07-12}}\n\n==Background==\nA [[Bantamweight (MMA)|bantamweight]] bout between [[Pedro Munhoz]] and former [[UFC They also announced that Jones' victory was overturned to a no contest due to the violation. Vegas 15''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on November 28, 2020, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/ufc-announces-upcoming-event-schedule-through-november|title=UFC announces Heavyweight Championship]] challenger [[Derrick Lewis]] was expected to serve as the event headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/08/curtis-blaydes-vs-derrick-lewis-headline-ufc-event-nov-28|title=Curtis Blaydes vs. Derrick Lewis set to headline UFC event on Nov. 28|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Mike Bohn|date=2020-08-18|access-date=2020-08-19}} However, Blaydes [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]] a day before the event and the bout was cancelled.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/11/curtis-blaydes-tests-positive-covid-19-ufc-on-espn-18-main-event-canceled|title=Curtis Blaydes tests positive for the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{cite web|url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/2020-ufc-event-schedule-deiveson-figueiredo-vs-alex-perez-curtis-blaydes-vs-derrick-lewis-among-events/|title=2020 UFC event schedule: Deiveson Figueiredo middleweight bout between former [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] challenger [[Darren Till]] and [[Jack Hermansson]] to serve as the event headliner.{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/29712748/darren-jack-hermansson-headline-ufc-fight-night-dec-5|title=Till-Hermansson to headline December UFC card|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2020-08-21|access-date=2020-08-22}} However, it was revealed on November 6 that Till was forced to withdraw from the planned bout due to an undisclosed injury. He was replaced by [[Kevin Holland (fighter)|Kevin Holland]].{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/30268526/injured-darren-fight-jack-hermansson-ufc-fight-night|title=Injured Darren Till Okamoto|date=2020-09-04|access-date=2020-09-05}}\n\n==Background==\n[[Behind closed doors (sport)|Without fans in attendance]], the promotion did not have to worry about the local timing of the event, so the plan was to proceed with normal timing for [[prime time]] hours on the [[Eastern Time Zone|east coast]] of North America. The main card was scheduled to begin at 4:00 am (October 11) local time in Abu Dhabi, with a full preliminary card beginning at approximately 1:00 am [[Gulf Standard Time]].{{cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/ufc-fight-island-where-ufc-fight-island-fight-island-schedule-and-more|title=UFC Fight Island - Everything you need to know|publisher=ufc.com|author=Press Release|date=2020-09-17|access-date=2020-09-17}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between former [[WSOF Bantamweight Championship|WSOF Bantamweight Champion]] and [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] challenger [[Marlon Moraes]] and [[Cory Sandhagen]] served as the event headliner.{{cite news|url=https://globoesporte.globo.com/combate/noticia/ultimate-mira-marlon-moraes-x-cory-sandhagen-para-o-card-do-dia-10-de-outubro.ghtml|title=UFC targets Marlon Moraes vs. Cory Sandhagen for the October 10th card|publisher=globoesporte.globo.com|author=Raphael Marinho|access-date=2020-08-06|date=2020-08-06}} {{in lang|pt}}\n\nA featherweight bout between [[Edson Barboza]] and [[Jeremy Stephens]] was briefly linked to the event.{{cite news|url=https://globoesporte.globo.com/combate/noticia/ultimate-acerta-edson-barboza-x-jeremy-stephens-para-evento-do-dia-10-de-outubro.ghtml|title=UFC plans Edson Barboza vs. Jeremy Stephens for October 10th event|publisher=globoesporte.globo.com|author=Raphael Marinho|date=2020-08-06|access-date=2020-08-17}} {{in lang|pt}} However, Stephens declined the fight after indicating he did not feel the date allowed him adequate time to prepare and safely make the required weight. Barboza was scheduled to face [[Sodiq Yusuff]] instead.{{cite news|url=https://globoesporte.globo.com/combate/noticia/ultimate-muda-planos-e-edson-barboza-enfrenta-sodiq-yusuff-em-outubro.ghtmll|title=UFC changes plans, and Edson Barboza faces Sodiq Yusuff in October|publisher=globoesporte.globo.com|author=Raphael Marinho|date=2020-08-16|access-date=2020-08-17}} {{in lang|pt}} In turn, Yusuff pulled out of the fight on September 21 due to undisclosed reasons.{{cite web|url=https://www.uol.com.br/esporte/ultimas-noticias/ag-fight/2020/09/21/sem-rival-edson-barboza-agurda-definicao-do-ufc-para-duelo-na-ilha-da-luta.htm|title=Without an opponent Edson Barboza awaits definition from the UFC for duel at Fight Island|publisher=uol.com.br|author=Staff|date=2020-09-21|access-date=2020-09-22}} {{in lang|pt}} He was replaced by [[Makwan Amirkhani]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/09/sodiq-yusuff-out-edson-barboza-vs-makwan-amirkhani-featherweight-ufc-oct-10|title=With Sodiq Yusuff out, Edson Barboza meets Makwan Amirkhani on UFC's Oct. 10 card|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn and Nolan King|date=2020-09-27|access-date=2020-09-27}}\n\nA flyweight bout between promotional newcomer [[Tagir Ulanbekov]] and [[Bruno Gustavo da Silva]] was expected to take place four weeks earlier at [[UFC Fight Night: Waterson vs. Hill]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/7/18/21329075/tagir-ulanbekov-vs-bruno-silva-slated-for-ufc-event-on-sept-12|title=Tagir Ulanbekov vs. Bruno Silva slated for UFC event on Sept. 12|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Newswire|date=2020-07-18|access-date=2020-09-09}} However, due to [[travel restrictions related to the COVID-19 pandemic]], the pairing was rescheduled and took place at this event.{{cite web|url=https://www.gnp1.de/mma/mma-deutschland/news/wer-kaempft-gegen-wen-kw-36-2020/|title=Who is fighting against whom? KW 36/2020|publisher=gnp1.de|author=Alexander Petzel-Gligorea|date=2020-09-06|access-date=2020-09-09}} {{in lang|de}}\n\n[[Rodolfo Vieira]] was expected to face [[Markus Perez]] in a middleweight bout at the event.{{cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/bjj-specialist-rodolfo-vieira-treft-markus-perez-op-10-oktober/|title=BJJ specialist Rodolfo Vieira meets Markus Perez on October 10|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2020-09-06|access-date=2020-09-09}} {{in lang|nl}} However, Vieira pulled out on September 21 due to an injury sustained during training.{{cite He was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Ilia Topuria]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/10/seung-woo-choi-out-youssef-zalal-meets-ilia-topuria-at-ufc-on-espn-37|title=With Seung Woo Choi out, Youssef Zalal meets Ilia Topuria at UFC on ESPN+ 37|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-10-02|access-date=2020-10-02}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Serghei Spivac]] and [[Tom Aspinall]] was expected to take place at the event. However, it was announced on October 2 that Pérez had been forced out of the bout after he [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]].{{cite that took place on December 12, 2020 at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.si.com/mma/2020/08/21/amanda-nunes-defend-title-megan-anderson-ufc-256|title=Report: Amanda Nunes to defend featherweight title in December bout vs. Megan Anderson|publisher=si.com|author=Nick Selbe|date=2020-08-21|access-date=2020-08-21}}{{cite web|url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/2020-ufc-event-schedule-deiveson-figueiredo-vs-alex-perez-curtis-blaydes-vs-derrick-lewis-among-events/|title=2020 UFC event schedule: Deiveson Figueiredo vs. Alex Perez, Curtis Blaydes vs. Derrick Lewis among events|publisher=cbssports.com|author=Brent Brookhouse|date=2020-11-15|access-date=2020-11-19}}\n\n==Background==\nThe promotion was initially targeting a [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Kamaru Usman]] (also [[The Ultimate Fighter: American Top Team vs. Blackzilians]] welterweight winner) and [[Gilbert Burns (fighter)|Gilbert Burns]] to serve as the event headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/9/1/21417296/kamaru-usman-vs-gilbert-burns-targeted-for-ufc-256-in-december|title=Kamaru Usman vs. Gilbert Burns targeted for UFC 256 in December|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2020-09-01|access-date=2020-09-02}} The pairing was previously scheduled to meet in July at [[UFC 251]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/06/ufc-251-kamaru-usman-vs-gilbert-burns-booked-to-headline|title=Kamaru Usman vs. Gilbert Burns headlines UFC 251 on 'Fight Island' in Abu Dhabi|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-06-09|access-date=2020-09-02}} However, Burns was pulled from the contest after he and his coach, Greg Jones, [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/ufc-251-gilbert-burns-out-kamaru-usman-title-fight-coronavirus|title=Gilbert Burns tests positive for COVID-19, out of UFC 251 headliner vs. Kamaru Usman|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2020-07-03|access-date=2020-09-02}} In turn, Usman faced, and defeated [[Jorge Masvidal]] at that event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/29449667/kamaru-usman-beats-jorge-masvidal-via-unanimous-decision-ufc-251-retain-welterweight-belt|title=Kamaru Usman beats Jorge Masvidal via unanimous decision at UFC 251 to retain welterweight belt|work=espn.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2020-07-12|access-date=2020-09-02}} On October 5, it was reported Usman pulled out from this bout, citing more time needed to recover from undisclosed injuries and the bout was postponed.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/30048745/sources-kamaru-usman-gilbert-burns-fight-happen-dec-12|title=Sources: Usman-Burns fight on Dec. 12 is off|work=espn.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2020-10-05|access-date=2020-10-06}}\n\nWhile not officially announced by the organization, the promotion was also targeting a [[UFC Women's Featherweight Championship]] bout between the current champion [[Amanda Nunes]] (also the current [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship|UFC Women's Bantamweight Champion]]) and former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Featherweight Championship|Invicta FC Featherweight Champion]] [[Megan Anderson (fighter)|Megan Anderson]] to serve as the event headliner. In turn, it was announced on November 9 that Nunes pulled out due to an undisclosed injury and the bout was postponed to 2021.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/11/megan-anderson-vs-amanda-nunes-off-ufc-256-title-fight-pushed-2021-injury|title=Megan Anderson says Amanda Nunes out of UFC 256, title fight pushed to 2021|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2020-11-09|access-date=2020-11-09}}\n\nA [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Petr Yan]] and [[Aljamain Sterling]] was originally scheduled to serve as the co-main event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/10/ufc-256-petr-yan-vs-aljamain-sterling-bantamweight-title-fight|title=Petr Yan vs. Aljamain Sterling bantamweight title fight set for UFC 256|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2020-10-14|access-date=2020-10-14}} Due to the aforementioned cancelations, they were then expected to headline the card. On November 22, Russian media revealed that the fight was also canceled for personal reasons related to Yan and is expected to be booked at a later date.{{Cite web|url=https://tass.ru/sport/10069613|title=Yan vs. Sterling UFC title fight canceled|publisher=tass.ru|author=Staff|date=2020-11-22|access-date=2020-11-22}} {{in lang|ru}} Those \"personal reasons\" were later revealed to be related to [[travel visa|visa]] and travel issues.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/11/24/21612438/petr-yan-reveals-why-he-withdrew-from-scheduled-title-defense-against-aljamain-sterling-at-ufc-256|title=Morning Report: Petr Yan reveals why he withdrew from scheduled title defense against Aljamain Sterling at UFC 256|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Jed Meshew|date=2020-11-24|access-date=2020-11-24}}\n\nFollowing his first title defense at [[UFC 255]] on November 21, [[UFC Flyweight Championship|UFC Flyweight Champion]] [[Deiveson Figueiredo]] headlined this event against [[Brandon Moreno]], who also fought at that same date. This booking represented the shortest turnaround between title fights by a champion in UFC history at just 21 days.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/30363749/deiveson-figueiredo-brandon-moreno-agree-fight-ufc-256|title=Sources: Deiveson Figueiredo, Brandon Moreno agree to fight at UFC 256|work=espn.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2020-11-22|access-date=2020-11-22}}{{Cite 256|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff and Vinicius Giglio|date=2020-11-24|access-date=2020-11-24}} Former [[Rizin Fighting Federation#Bantamweight Championship|Rizin FF Bantamweight Champion]] [[Manel Kape]], who was expected to make his promotional debut a week later at [[UFC Fight Night: Thompson vs. Neal]], served as a potential replacement.{{Cite Fight Night: Thompson vs. Neal]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC on ESPN: Chiesa vs. Magny]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC on ABC: Holloway vs. Kattar''''' (also known as '''''UFC on ABC 1''''' and '''''UFC Fight Island 7''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on January 16, 2021, at the [[Etihad Arena]] on [[Yas Island]], [[Abu Dhabi]], United Arab Emirates.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/12/22/22195150/dana-white-announces-ufc-257-first-three-events-of-2021-to-take-place-on-fight-island|title=Dana White announces UFC 257, first three events of 2021 to take place on Fight Island|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2020-12-22|access-date=2020-12-22}}\n\n== Background ==\nThe main card marked the first time a mixed martial arts event was broadcast on [[American Broadcasting Company|ABC]]. It also meant the UFC's first return to network television since [[UFC on Fox: Lee vs. Iaquinta 2]] was broadcast on [[Fox Broadcasting Company|Fox]] in December 2018.{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/30650353/ufc-fight-night-max-holloway-vs-calvin-kattar-main-card-air-abc-network-television|title=UFC Fight Night: Max Holloway vs. Calvin Kattar main card to air on ABC network television|publisher=espn.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2021-01-04|access-date=2021-01-04}}\n\nAfter hosting nine events [[Behind closed doors (sport)|behind closed doors]] at the [[Yas Island#du Arena and Forum|du Forum]] amid the [[COVID-19 pandemic]], the UFC made its debut at the newly built [[Etihad Arena]]. A limited number of fans were allowed inside the venue, marking the first time since [[UFC 248]] in March 2020 that non-essential event personnel were in attendance.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/01/ufc-limited-fans-attendance-etihad-arena-fight-island-mma-conor-mcgregor-dustin-poirier|title=Fans are back: UFC will have 'limited' attendance at three-card 'Fight Island' stint at Etihad Arena|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2021-01-07|access-date=2021-01-07}} The venue has maximum capacity of over 18,000, but the UFC is expecting to have closer to 2,000 fans for each of the Fight Island's events during the week.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/1/10/22223333/etihad-arena-lists-official-covid-19-safety-measures-for-fans-at-january-ufc-fight-island-shows|title=Etihad Arena lists official COVID-19 safety measures for fans at January UFC Fight Island shows|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Newswire|date=2021-01-10|access-date=2021-01-11}}\n\nA featherweight bout between former [[UFC Featherweight Championship|UFC Featherweight Champion]] [[Max Holloway]] and [[Calvin Kattar]] served as the event headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/11/max-holloway-vs-calvin-kattar-featherweight-main-event-fight-night-jan-16|title=Max Holloway vs. Calvin Kattar headlines UFC Fight Night on Jan. 16|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2020-11-17|access-date=2020-11-17}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Ricky Simon]] and [[Brian Kelleher]] was initially scheduled to take place at [[UFC Fight Night: Overeem vs. Sakai]], but Simon's cornerman [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]] and he was forced to withdraw from the event.{{Cite '''''UFC Vegas 12''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on October 31, 2020 at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/ufc-announces-upcoming-event-schedule-through-november|title=UFC announces upcoming event schedule through November|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=2020-10-07|access-date=2020-10-07}}\n\n==Background==\n[[File:Anderson Silva.png|thumb|left|180px|UFC president [[Dana White]] announced that this would be [[Anderson Silva]]'s final fight in the UFC. He is considered one of the greatest of all time and still holds the record for [[List of UFC records#Most consecutive wins|most consecutive wins in the organization]] (16) and [[List of UFC records#Longest single title reigns|longest title reign in UFC history]] (2,457 days).{{cite web|url=https://www.sportbible.com/ufc/news-legends-mma-dana-white-confirms-ufc-legend-anderson-silvas-final-career-opponent-20200826|title=Dana White confirms UFC legend Anderson Silva's final career opponent|publisher=sportbible.com|author=Daniel Marland|date=2020-08-26|access-date=2020-10-13}}{{cite last fight'|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2020-10-20|access-date=2020-10-21}}]]\n\nA middleweight bout between former [[UFC Middleweight Championship|UFC Middleweight Champion]] [[Anderson Silva]] and [[Uriah Hall]] served as the headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/29693191/anderson-silva-uriah-hall-fight-oct-31-dana-white-says|title=Anderson Silva, Uriah Hall to fight Oct. 31, Dana White says|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2020-08-19|access-date=2020-08-19}} The pairing was initially scheduled to meet at [[UFC 198]] in May 2016.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/3/16/11251410/anderson-silva-vs-uriah-hall-targeted-for-ufc-198|title=Anderson Silva vs. Uriah Hall targeted for UFC 198|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2016-03-16|access-date=2020-08-19}} However, Silva pulled out due to [[acute cholecystitis]], which required a [[cholecystectomy]].{{Cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2016/05/reps-anderson-silva-undergoes-successful-surgery-after-ufc-198-withdrawal|title=Reps: Anderson Silva undergoes successful surgery after UFC 198 withdrawal (Updated)|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Fernanda Prates and Dann Stupp|date=2016-05-11|access-date=2020-08-19}}\n\n[[Raulian Paiva]] was expected to face [[Amir Albazi]] in a flyweight bout at the event. However, Paiva pulled out of the fight on September 11 citing a knee injury.{{Cite Zhalgas Zhumagulov and their contest was expected to take place at [[UFC on ESPN: Smith vs. Clark]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/10/raulian-paiva-out-zhalgas-zhumagulov-vs-amir-albazi-ufc-november-28-fight-booking-mma|title=With Raulian Paiva out, Zhalgas Zhumagulov steps in to face middleweight bout between [[Wellington Turman]] and [[Sean Strickland]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/sean-strickland-vs-wellington-turman-toegevoegd-aan-ufc-halloween-evenement/|title=Sean Strickland vs. Wellington Turman added to \"UFC Halloween\" training after his two-week quarantine period for [[Coronavirus disease 2019|the disease]] ended on September 24.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/9/29/21493762/wellington-turman-covid-19-ufc-fight-sean-strickland-augusto-sakai-rogerio-bontorin|title=Wellington Turman still unable to train after COVID-19 Williams weighed in at 175.5 pounds, four and a half pounds over the welterweight non-title fight limit. Both of their bouts proceeded at catchweight and they were each fined a percentage of their individual purses, which went to their opponents Sean Strickland and Jason Witt.{{Cite Alexander Hernandez, Adrian Yañez''', and '''Miles Johns'''\n\n==Aftermath==\nOn February 9, 2021, it was announced that the [[Nevada State Athletic Commission]] (NSAC) issued a six-month suspension and $2,245.36 ($1,800 + $445.36 in legal fees) fine for Charlie Ontiveros due to a failed drug test related to this event. His results showed the presence of [[dehydrochloromethyltestosterone|DHCMT]] M3 metabolites.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/02/ufc-news-charlie-ontiveros-suspended-nac-failed-drug-test|title=NAC suspends UFC's Charlie Ontiveros six months for failed drug
