Fight Night: Felder vs. dos Anjos]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC on ESPN: Santos vs. Teixeira''''' (also known as '''''UFC on ESPN 17''''' and '''''UFC Vegas 13''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on November 7, 2020 at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/ufc-announces-upcoming-event-schedule-through-november|title=UFC announces upcoming event schedule through November|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=2020-10-07|access-date=2020-10-07}}\n\n==Background==\nA light heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] challengers [[Thiago Santos]] and [[Glover Teixeira]] headlined this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/09/ufc-news-thiago-santos-glover-teixeira-light-heavyweight-main-event-nov-7-las-vegas|title=Third time's the charm? UFC rebooks Thiago Santos vs. Glover Teixeira for Nov. 7 headliner|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Mike Bohn|date=2020-09-24|access-date=2020-09-24}} They were initially scheduled to headline [[UFC Fight Night: Waterson vs. Hill]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/thiago-santos-vs-glover-teixeira-light-heavyweight-ufc-main-event-sept-12|title=Thiago Santos vs. Glover Teixeira in the works to headline UFC's Sept. 12 event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2020-07-07|access-date=2020-07-14}} However, the pairing was rescheduled for [[UFC on ESPN: Holm vs. Aldana]] after Teixeira [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]] a week prior to the fight.{{Cite web|title=Glover Teixeira tests positive for COVID-19, fight against Thiago Santos rescheduled for UFC event on Oct. 3|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/9/3/21419531/glover-teixeira-tests-positive-covid-19-fight-against-thiago-santos-rescheduled-ufc-event-on-oct-3|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2020-09-03|access-date=2020-09-03}} On September 15, the bout was postponed again as Santos tested positive for [[Coronavirus disease 2019|the disease]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/9/15/21438717/thiago-santos-tests-positive-for-covid-19-oct-3-fight-against-glover-teixeira-canceled|title=Thiago Santos tests positive for COVID-19, Oct. 3 fight against Glover Teixeira canceled|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee and Guilherme Cruz|date=2020-09-15|access-date=2020-09-15}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Ian Heinisch]] and [[Brendan Allen]] was previously scheduled to take place earlier this year at [[UFC on ESPN: Poirier vs. Hooker]]. However, Heinisch pulled out due to injury and the matchup was scrapped.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/6/16/21293607/ian-heinisch-out-brendan-allen-now-expected-to-face-ufc-newcomer-kyle-daukaus-on-june-27|title=Ian Heinisch out; Brendan Allen now expected to face UFC newcomer Kyle Daukaus on June 27|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2020-06-16|access-date=2020-08-15}} The pairing was rescheduled and expected to take place at this event.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/8/14/21369523/ian-heinisch-vs-brendan-allen-rebooking-on-tap-for-nov-7-ufc-event|title=Ian Heinisch vs. Brendan Allen rebooking on tap for Nov. 7 UFC event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Newswire|date=2020-08-14|access-date=2020-08-15}} On the day of the event, the UFC announced the bout was once again cancelled due to a positive COVID-19 test involving Heinisch.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/11/7/21554371/ian-heinisch-vs-brendan-allen-ufc-vegas-13-fight-canceled-due-to-positive-covid-19-test|title=Ian Heinisch vs. Brendan Allen UFC Vegas 13 fight canceled due to positive COVID-19 test|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2020-11-07|access-date=2020-11-07}}\n\nA strawweight bout between former [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship]] challenger [[Cláudia Gadelha]] and [[Yan Xiaonan]] was expected to take place at [[UFC 253]], but a knee injury sustained by Gadelha ruled her out of the bout.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/9/1/21410067/knee-injury-claudia-gadelha-out-ufc-253-fight-yan-xiaonan|title=Knee injury forces Claudia Gadelha out of UFC 253 fight with Yan Xiaonan|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2020-09-01|access-date=2020-09-01}} The pairing was rescheduled for this event instead.{{cite web|url=http://www.china.org.cn/sports/2020-09/10/content_76688797.htm|title=Yan sticking to master plan|publisher=china.org.cn|author=Staff|date=2020-09-10|access-date=2020-09-10}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Carlos Diego Ferreira]] and [[Drew Dober]] was initially scheduled to take place in May at [[UFC Fight Night: Hermansson vs. Weidman]]. However, the event was cancelled due to the [[COVID-19 pandemic]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/4/20/21228718/ufc-officially-cancels-four-events-due-to-coronavirus-pandemic|title=UFC officially cancels four events due to coronavirus pandemic|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2020-04-20|access-date=2020-04-20}} The pairing was then rescheduled for this event.{{Cite web|title=Diego Ferreira faces Drew Dober on November 7 UFC card|url=https://www.fightful.com/mma/report-diego-ferreira-faces-drew-dober-november-7-ufc-card|publisher=fightful.com|author=David Tees|date=2020-09-12|access-date=2020-09-14}} In turn, Ferreira pulled out on October 22 due to an illness and the bout was scrapped.{{Cite web|url=https://mymmanews.com/diego-ferreira-out-of-drew-dober-fight-due-to-illness/|title=Diego Ferreira out of Drew Dober fight due to illness|publisher=mymmanews.com|author=Blaine Henry|date=2020-10-22|access-date=2020-10-22}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Heavyweight Championship|UFC Heavyweight Champion]] [[Andrei Arlovski]] and [[Tanner Boser]] was briefly scheduled for [[UFC on ESPN: Holm vs. Aldana]], but was later moved to this event due to Arlovski being ill.{{Cite web|url=https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2020/9/16/21440543/ufc-news-tanner-boser-vs-andrei-arlovski-reported-moved-to-november-fight-card|title=Tanner Boser vs. Andrei Arlovski reportedly moved to November UFC Fight Night card|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Kristen King|date=2020-09-16|access-date=2020-09-24|archive-date=2020-09-19|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20200919062821/https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2020/9/16/21440543/ufc-news-tanner-boser-vs-andrei-arlovski-reported-moved-to-november-fight-card|url-status=dead}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Marcos Rogério de Lima]] and [[Alexander Romanov (fighter)|Alexander Romanov]] was initially scheduled to take place a month earlier at [[UFC Fight Night: Overeem vs. Sakai]]. However, Lima pulled out after testing positive for COVID-19.{{Cite web|url=https://globoesporte.globo.com/combate/noticia/marcos-pezao-testa-positivo-para-covid-19-e-deixa-card-do-ufc-overeem-x-sakai.ghtml|title=Marcos Pezão tests positive for COVID-19 and leaves the UFC: Overeem vs. Sakai card|publisher=globoesporte.globo.com|author=Ana Hissa|date=2020-09-05|access-date=2020-09-05}} {{in lang|pt}} The pairing took place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/10/1/21496934/marcos-rogerio-de-lima-alexander-romanov-rebooked-november-7-ufc-event|title=Marcos Rogerio de Lima vs. Alexander Romanov re-booked for Nov. 7 UFC event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2020-10-01|access-date=2020-10-01}}\n\n[[Philipe Lins]] was expected to face [[Don'Tale Mayes]] in a heavyweight bout at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/10/1/21497429/ufc-philipe-lins-dontale-mayes-november-7-card-las-vegas|title=UFC adds Philipe Lins vs. Don’Tale Mayes to Nov. 7 card in Las Vegas|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2020-10-01|access-date=2020-10-02}} However, it was announced on October 14 that Lins had a knee injury and pulled out of the event. Mayes was then expected to face [[Roque Martinez]] a week later at [[UFC Fight Night: Felder vs. dos Anjos]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/10/philipe-lins-out-ufc-books-roque-martinez-vs-dontale-mayes-november-14|title=With Philipe Lins out, UFC shifts Don'Tale Mayes onto Nov. 14 card vs. Roque Martinez|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2020-10-14|access-date=2020-10-14}}\n\nA featherweight bout between [[Jeremy Stephens]] and [[Arnold Allen]] was scheduled to take place at the event. However, Stephens was forced to withdraw from the event in late October citing injury. A replacement could not be found and Allen was also removed.{{Cite web|title=Jeremy Stephens injured, out of UFC fight vs. Arnold Allen on Nov. 7|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/10/ufc-news-jeremy-stephens-injured-out-november-7-vs-arnold-allen|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2020-10-24|access-date=2020-10-25}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Khalid Taha]] and [[Jack Shore]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/jack-shore-en-khalid-taha-treffen-elkaar-op-7-november/|title=Jack Shore and Khalid Taha will meet on November 7|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Staff|date=2020-09-27|access-date=2020-10-28}} {{in lang|nl}} However, Shore was removed from the bout in late October due to undisclosed reasons and replaced by [[Raoni Barcelos]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/10/jack-shore-out-raoni-barcelos-vs-khalid-taha-ufc-espn-plus-40-mma-fight-booking|title=With Jack Shore out, Raoni Barcelos steps in to face Khalid Taha at UFC on ESPN+ 40|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-10-28|access-date=2020-10-28}}\n\n[[Felipe Colares]] pulled out of a scheduled bantamweight bout against [[Gustavo Lopez (fighter)|Gustavo Lopez]] during fight week as he tested positive for COVID-19.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/11/1/21544661/felipe-colares-tests-positive-covid-19-out-ufc-vegas-13-fight-gustavo-lopez|title=Felipe Colares tests positive for COVID-19, out of UFC Vegas 13 fight with Gustavo Lopez|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2020-11-01|access-date=2020-11-03}} He was replaced by returning veteran [[Anthony Birchak]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/11/ufc-on-espn-plus-40-anthony-birchak-returns-gustavo-lopez|title=Anthony Birchak's UFC return vs. Gustavo Lopez finalizes UFC on ESPN+ 40 lineup|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2020-11-03|access-date=2020-11-05}}\n\nAnother bout affected by the COVID-19 pandemic was a bantamweight contest between [[Sarah Moras]] and [[Vanessa Melo]], as Moras tested positive for the disease and the pairing was postponed to take place at [[UFC on ABC: Holloway vs. Kattar]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/8/13/21368026/sarah-moras-vs-vanessa-melo-set-for-nov-7-ufc-event|title=Sarah Moras vs. Vanessa Melo set for Nov. 7 UFC event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz and Mike Heck|date=2020-08-13|access-date=2021-01-15}}{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2021/01/14/ufc-fight-island-7s-sarah-moras-homesick-and-ready-to-hurt-someone/|title=UFC Fight Island 7's Sarah Moras: homesick and ready to hurt someone|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Daniel Vreeland|date=2021-01-14|access-date=2021-01-15}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card (ESPN2 / ESPN+)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Light [[UFC 256|UFC 256: Figueiredo vs. Moreno]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC on ABC: Holloway vs. Kattar]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Thompson vs. Neal''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 183''''', '''''UFC on ESPN+ 41''''' and '''''UFC Vegas 17''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on December 19, 2020 at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{cite web|url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/2020-ufc-event-schedule-deiveson-figueiredo-vs-alex-perez-curtis-blaydes-vs-derrick-lewis-among-events/|title=2020 UFC event schedule: Deiveson Figueiredo vs. Alex Perez, Curtis Blaydes vs. Derrick Lewis among events|publisher=cbssports.com|author=Brent Brookhouse|date=2020-11-15|access-date=2020-11-19}}\n\n==Background==\nA welterweight bout between [[Leon Edwards]] and [[Khamzat Chimaev]] was initially scheduled to headline this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/10/ufc-news-dana-white-announces-leon-edwards-khamzat-chimaev|title=UFC books Leon Edwards vs. Khamzat Chimaev, expected to headline Dec. 19|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2020-10-23|access-date=2020-10-23}} On November 29, it was announced that Chimaev had [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]], but was expected to be able to compete.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2020/11/29/21725459/report-khamzat-chimaev-has-covid-19-december-19-fight-in-jeopardy|title=Khamzat Chimaev has COVID-19, December 19 fight in jeopardy|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Ryan Harkness|date=2020-11-29|access-date=2020-12-01}} In turn, Edwards pulled out two days later after also testing positive. His case was worse than Chimaev's and he was still suffering from severe symptoms of the [[Coronavirus disease 2019|disease]]. The promotion was expected to keep the bout intact and reschedule the pairing for a future event in early 2021, but Chimaev's lingering symptoms prevented him from returning until [[UFC 267]].{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/30429656/sources-leon-edwards-khamzat-chimaev-fight-edwards-tests-positive-covid-19%3fplatform=amp|title=Sources: Leon Edwards-Khamzat Chimaev fight off after Edwards tests positive for COVID-19|work=espn.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2020-12-01|access-date=2020-12-01}} \n\nThe original co-main event, a welterweight bout between former [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] challenger [[Stephen Thompson (fighter)|Stephen Thompson]] and [[Geoff Neal]] served as the event headliner.{{cite web|url=https://www.thescore.com/mma/news/2062346|title=Report: Thompson-Neal moved to Dec. 19 main event|publisher=thescore.com|author=Nick Baldwin|date=2020-12-01|access-date=2020-12-01}}\n\nA flyweight bout between [[Matt Schnell]] and [[Tyson Nam]] was expected to take place at the event.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/10/matt-schnell-vs-tyson-nam-ufc-mma-fight-booking-december-19s|title=UFC books Matt Schnell vs. Tyson Nam for Dec. 19 event in Las Vegas|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2020-10-25|access-date=2020-10-25}}{{Dead link|date=July 2025 |bot=InternetArchiveBot |fix-attempted=yes }} The pairing was previously scheduled to take place in September at [[UFC Fight Night: Waterson vs. Hill]].{{Cite web|date=2020-07-03|title=Matt Schnell vs. Tyson Nam joins UFC's September 12 lineup|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/07/matt-schnell-vs-tyson-nam-joins-ufcs-september-12-lineup|access-date=2020-07-22|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|language=en}} However, the fight was canceled on the day of the event's weigh-in as Schnell was deemed medically unfit to compete due to complications related to his weight cut.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/09/ufc-on-espn-plus-35-michelle-waterson-angela-hill-weigh-in-results|title=Weight-cutting issues scrap Tyson Nam from Matt Schnell fight at UFC on ESPN+ 35|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2020-09-11|access-date=2020-09-11}} The bout was subsequently rescheduled for [[UFC on ESPN 20]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/12/matt-schnell-tyson-nam-rebooked-ufc-fight-night-jan-20|title=Matt Schnell vs. Tyson Nam rebooked for UFC Fight Night on Jan. 20|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun and Nolan King|date=2020-12-11|access-date=2020-12-11}}\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Belal Muhammad]] and [[Sean Brady (fighter)|Sean Brady]] was expected to take place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/10/belal-muhammad-vs-sean-brady-at-ufc-december-19|title=Belal Muhammad vs. Sean Brady added to UFC's Dec. 19 event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-10-07|access-date=2020-10-07}} However in late October, Muhammad revealed that Brady had a broken nose and had to pull out of their bout.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/10/belal-muhammad-says-sean-brady-out-wants-mike-perry-ufc-december-19|title=Belal Muhammad says Sean Brady is out of their fight, wants Mike Perry as a replacement|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-10-30|access-date=2020-10-30}} He was replaced by [[Dhiego Lima]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2020/11/ufc-news-sean-brady-out-belal-muhammad-vs-dhiego-lima-set|title=With Sean Brady out, Belal Muhammad faces Dhiego Lima at UFC Fight Night on Dec. 19|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2020-11-16|access-date=2020-11-17}} Subsequently, Muhammad was diagnosed with COVID-19 during the week leading up to the event and the bout was scrapped. marking the first time since [[UFC 248]] in March 2020 that non-essential event personnel were in attendance.{{cite
